She opened her eyes. The world swam. She found {A} in the blur, standing, waiting, and {B}, still curled round her hurt and still shaking, made herself hold her stare.

== collapse
{B} had been standing so long on will alone that she did not notice when the will ran out.

{A} had stepped back to breathe. The fighting had paused, the way it sometimes did, both of them drawing air, both of them measuring. {B} was measuring too. She was looking at {A} across the trampled ground and choosing her next move, and she had chosen it, and she was ready to go.

== knockout
It was not even the hardest blow of the fight. It was simply the last one {B} had room for.

{A} felt it land and felt, in the same instant, the fight go out of the body in front of her. Not slowly. All at once, the way a light goes out. {B}'s eyes had been locked on her a moment before, hard and bright and furious, and now they were open and empty, looking past {A} at nothing.

== dwell
The blow was over, but the hurt had only just started arriving.

{B} had braced for it to land. She had not braced for this: for the way it kept coming after {A} had already stepped away. A first bright flash where it struck. == dwell
{B} stood very still and let it go through her, because there was nothing else to do with it.

The blow had landed on her shoulder, high, near the neck, and it had not been the hardest she had taken. She did not move. She stood with her head a little bowed and her weight a little off that side and breathed through it. Her breath was shaking. She noticed that and tried to steady it and could not quite.

A small trickle of something warm ran down from the hurt place. She felt it go. A thin thread, warm and slow, and then it stopped.

The ache was changing as she stood there. The sharp edge of it was dulling, sinking, turning into something deeper and heavier. A weight. She could feel the shoulder stiffening, the muscle going tight and hot round the bruise, and she knew that when she tried to move it would tell her about it.

She tried a little. It told her. A hot pull, all down the side, and her breath caught and came out as a short hiss.

*Fine. I know where you are now. Stay there.*

She lifted her head. Slowly. The ache went up into her neck with the movement and she let it.

{A} was a little way off, watching her with that patient, measuring look. {B} met it. Her shoulder burned and she did not let it show, and she let her breath out, slow and even, and set her feet.

== read
{A} was the one on the ground, and from there she could see more than {B} thought.

{B} stood over her at a careful distance, not closing in. She had the look of someone who believes she has time. One side of her face had swollen, the skin puffed and tight, and {A} watched her turn her head now and then to see round it. She did it without thinking. She had done it often enough already that it had become a habit.

Her breathing was even. Very even. {A} lay still and listened to it, and after a while she heard what she was listening for: a breath that stopped short, caught on something, and went on as if it had not. Then another, a little later. Then another.

*You're hurting too. You're just better at hiding it than I am.*

{B}'s eyes were on {A}'s side, the one that would not let her straighten properly. They stayed there, and there was something pleased and patient in them that made {A}'s skin tighten. She knew what that look meant. {B} was choosing.

{A} let her choose. She kept the hurt side where it was, plain to see, and did not move to cover it. Let {B} think that was all of it.

And she kept looking. {B}'s weight sat a shade heavier on one side than the other, not a limp, only a preference. The shoulder on that side rode a fraction high and tense, the way a shoulder does when it has been struck and expects to be struck again.

*And that, too. She's guarding more than she knows.*

{B} shifted her stance, a small resettling, and at the end of it her breath caught again, the same small snag. Her face did not change. {A} saw it anyway.

She lay still and let her own hurts ache, and stored every piece of it away: the face, the shoulder, the breath. Then, slowly, so slowly it might have been nothing, she began to draw herself together.

== read
{A} stopped circling and simply looked, and took her time about it.

{B} was not where she had been at the start of this. That much was plain. At the start she had stood loose and easy, her weight balanced, her eyes quick. Now everything about her had tightened. Her stance had narrowed. Her weight sat back, careful, as if she did not quite trust the ground. And her eyes, still hard, still steady, followed {A} with the faint slowness of someone who has to think about each movement before making it.

*She's stiffening up. The longer this goes, the worse it'll get for her.*

{A} looked closer. There, at the side, where {A} had landed the last good blow: {B} was holding herself round it. Not obviously. A slight curve to her body, a turning-away, so that the hurt side was a little further back than the other. Every time she breathed in, that curve deepened a fraction.

And her breathing. Quick, shallow, high in the chest. Each breath stopped before it reached the bottom.

*Can't get a full breath. She'll tire faster than I will.*

{A} shifted her own weight, slowly, to the left. {B}'s eyes followed. A beat later, her body followed too, turning to keep {A} in front, and the turn was careful, guarded, the hurt side kept back.

{A} shifted to the right. The same. Careful. Guarded. A half-beat slow.

She saw it now, the whole shape of it. {B} could turn one way easily and the other way only slowly, because the other way meant leading with the hurt side.

*So I'll come from the slow side.*

{A} let none of it show in her face. She took a slow step to the right, and another, and watched {B} turn to follow, slow, careful, and smiled inwardly, and kept circling.

== knockout
Wren came in low and fast, and Sorrel saw it a half-beat too late.

The blow caught her under the jaw. There was no pain in it, not at first; only a strange, bright silence, as if the clearing had been switched off. She felt her legs go and could not remember how to stop them. The ground came up sideways and met her shoulder, and then her cheek, and the grass was very close and very green.

She tried to lift her head. Nothing happened. Somewhere above her, a long way off, Wren was breathing hard.

Then even that went away.

Wren stood over her with her own legs shaking, waiting for Sorrel to roll, to kick, to do any of the hundred things she had done all fight. The still body did none of them. Its ribs rose, slow and shallow. Fell. Rose.

*Done,* Wren thought. *She's done.* And only then did she let herself feel how much everything hurt.

== aftermath
Sorrel came back to the sound of water.

For a while she did not know where she was or why her whole body was one long dull ache. Then her jaw throbbed, and the rest came back with it: the rush, the blow she had not seen, the grass rising to meet her. She opened her eyes. The sky was the wrong way up.

She tried to get her legs under her and managed one of them. It trembled and gave, and she lay still again and breathed, and let the ache tell her where she had been hurt.

Wren was sitting a little way off, head low, watching her. She did not move to finish anything. She did not need to.


== stagger take
The tail caught Wren high on the cheek, and her head went with it before the pain did, snapping round to the left so fast her ear slapped her own neck. The rest of her followed. One step, two, her paws skating on the wet stone, and then her shoulder found the cave wall and stopped her there.

She stayed against it for a heartbeat longer than she should have. Her jaw throbbed, a hot, swelling ache along the bone, and the cave had a slow tilt to it that wouldn't settle.

*Off the wall. Get off the wall.*

She pushed away from it with her guard still half down, and Sorrel was already coming.

== jolted take
Sorrel's grip on the back of her neck didn't move, and that was the worst of it: every blow had nowhere to go. The first one rocked Wren's head down toward the sand and the hold yanked it back. The second came in under her ribs, and her whole body bucked against the arm pinning her, and the arm took it, and held.

The third she barely felt. Her head lolled with it, then came back up, slower.

== jolted take
Wren lay on her back in the grit and the blows came down. There was nowhere to roll with them. Each one drove into her chest and then the ground drove back, up through her shoulders and the back of her head, so that every hit landed twice and the second one was the stone's.

By the third she had stopped trying to twist away and only turned her face, and the floor scraped her cheek.

== off target
Sorrel went for the throat. She had seen it, the pale fur under Wren's jaw, bare for half a heartbeat as Wren looked up, and she went for it with everything she had.

Wren's chin came down. Not a guard, not even a choice: her head just ducked, the way a head does when something comes at it, and Sorrel's teeth closed on the thick fur of her shoulder instead, a hand's width from where they had been sent.

It still hurt. Wren yelped and wrenched away, and Sorrel let her go with a mouthful of fur and the taste of a chance that had been there and was gone.

# ===============================================================================================================
# BUILD 176: SELLING THE HIT, for every kind of body and attack. Blows on a fighter who cannot move with them
# (pinned take), on a fighter who is worn down overall (strike take worn), and the body's struggle with a hurt
# part afterwards. Conditions after the tags keep each passage to fights where its bodies and attacks exist.
# ===============================================================================================================

== pinned take
{A}'s whole weight was on {B}'s shoulders, pressing her into the ground, and {B} had stopped trying to buck it off for the moment, only breathing, ribs working under the fur. She saw the strike coming in the corner of her eye and could do nothing about it. There was nowhere to go.

It took her in the side, just under the ribs.

On her feet she would have gone with it, rolled away, let the ground have half of it. Pinned, she had to take all of it. The blow went in and stopped in her, and the shock had nowhere to go but through: up her side, into her chest, along her spine to the back of her skull, a single jolt that lit every nerve on the way. Her body jerked against the weight holding it and the weight did not move.

Her mouth fell open. No sound came out at first, only the gasp, long and dragging, the breath sucked in through her teeth as if the air had been punched out the other side of her. Her eyes squeezed shut so hard the fur round them creased.

Then the sound came: a thin, strangled cry that cracked in the middle.

She lay under {A} with her side burning and her flank twitching on its own, little jumps of the muscle under the fur where the blow had gone in. Every breath pulled at it. She took them short and shallow and fast, her mouth still open, her eyes still shut, and felt the next blow coming in the way {A}'s weight shifted on her back.

== pinned take, jolted take | has_b: wings
{B} lay on her front in the dirt with one wing pinned out flat beneath {A}'s foot and the other crushed against her own side, and she could feel the feathers of it bending under the weight.

{A} drove down into her ribs.

Pinned, there was no flinching from it. The blow struck her side and the force ran straight up through her body and out along the trapped wing, and every feather on it shuddered at once and then lay still. Her free wing jerked open on its own, a single convulsive beat that lifted dust off the ground and went nowhere, and folded back against her half-closed, the long feathers at its tip trembling.

A gasp tore out of her, beak wide. Her eyes squeezed shut and stayed shut. Her crest flattened to her skull.

*Up. Get up. Wings. Get the wings under you.*

She tried. The pinned wing would not come; the weight on it was too much, and the effort only ground it harder into the dirt and sent a hot, wrenching ache along the joint where it met her shoulder. She stopped trying and lay panting, beak open, every breath a short, whistling rasp against the ground, while her flank shook in small, steady jerks where the blow had gone in.

== pinned take, grab take | has_a: coils
{A} had her wrapped twice round the middle and once round the shoulders, and the coils tightened every time {B} breathed out, so that each breath in was a little smaller than the last.

{B} could not see the strike. She felt the coils shift against her ribs, the scales sliding as {A} drew her head back, and then the blow came down into her side, in the narrow gap between two loops of {A}'s body.

She could not move with it. The coils held her as still as stone, and the whole force of it went into her and stayed there: through the ribs, into her belly, a deep, dull shock that rolled up into her chest and lodged under her breastbone. Her body tried to curl round the place and the coils would not let it, and the trying hurt nearly as much as the blow.

Her mouth gaped. The gasp went in and came straight back out as a groan, squeezed out of her by the next tightening of the coils.

She could feel her own heartbeat against {A}'s scales, fast and hard, and the side where she had been struck throbbing in time with it. She shut her eyes. Her paws scrabbled at the coils, found nothing to grip on the smooth scales, and fell still again, trembling.

== pinned take | kind: electric
{B} was down on her side, {A} kneeling on her shoulder and hip, and the first she knew of the charge was the smell of it: sharp, hot, like the air after lightning.

{A} laid a crackling paw flat against her ribs and let go.

Standing, she could have jumped from it; the current would have bitten and passed. Pinned, it had nowhere to go but into her. It came in through her side and went everywhere at once, along every limb to the tips of her toes, up her spine into her jaw, so that her teeth clamped together on their own and her whole body locked rigid under {A}'s weight. Every hair on her stood up and crackled. Her legs kicked out straight and shook there.

It lasted a heartbeat. It felt much longer.

When it let go of her she sagged into the ground, gasping, mouth wide, eyes screwed shut. Her muscles kept jumping on their own: a twitch in her shoulder, a flutter in her flank, her paw clenching and unclenching against the dirt. The place on her side where the paw had been burned and tingled, and her own heart felt wrong in her chest, stumbling and then catching up.

She breathed, and breathed, and the smell of scorched fur came in with every breath.

== pinned take | kind: fire
{B} lay on her back with {A} standing over her, one heavy foot planted on her chest, and she could feel the heat coming off {A} before she saw the fire.

It poured down onto her belly.

On her feet she would have twisted from it, rolled through it, put it out against the ground. On her back, held, there was no rolling. The fire washed over her stomach and her flank and she could do nothing but feel it: the first shock of heat, then the deeper ache as it soaked in, then the sting that spread out across her side and kept spreading after the flames were gone.

She arched up off the ground against the weight on her chest. It did not give. Her mouth opened on a cry that came out high and cracked, and her eyes squeezed shut against the light of the flames, and she turned her head away as if that could help.

When it stopped, she lay panting with her mouth open, every breath shallow and quick, her belly fur singed and curling, her side twitching. A thin trembling had started in her legs and would not stop. She did not open her eyes. She could still feel the heat in her, under the skin, slowly going deeper.

== strike take worn
{B} had been hit so many times that the blows had started to arrive before she felt them.

This one took her on the shoulder, not hard, not as hard as some, and still it nearly folded her. Her knees dipped. Her breath went out of her in a long, low groan that she would not have let out an hour ago, and her eyes slid shut on it.

She had nothing left to meet it with. That was the difference. At the start of the fight her body had answered every blow with a blow of its own, a set of the shoulders, a snarl. Now it simply received them. The shock went in and found no resistance and spread out through her, a slow ripple that set her whole frame trembling.

She put a paw to the shoulder and pressed. Under the fur she could feel her own pulse jumping.

*Breathe.* She tried. The breath came in shallow and fast and caught at the top, and the next one did the same, and she stood there bent a little round her own shoulder, mouth open, panting, while the trembling in her legs went on and on.

When she opened her eyes, the world swam for a moment before it came back. {A} was still there.

== strike take worn | has_b: coils, scales
{B}'s coils had lost their shape. Earlier in the fight she had held herself in tight, gleaming spirals, her head high above them, swaying. Now her body lay in loose, uneven loops on the ground, and her head had sunk until it was barely higher than {A}'s.

The cold struck her full along the side.

Her scales went white with frost where it touched, and the white spread, and her body, which should have whipped away from it, only shuddered. A long, slow ripple ran from the place it struck down the whole length of her, through every loop, to the very tip of her tail, which twitched once and lay still.

A hiss came out of her, but it was a tired hiss, thin and leaking, with no fury left in it.

She could feel the cold going in. It went under the scales and into the muscle and made the muscle slow and stiff, so that when she tried to draw her coils back in, they came slowly, grudgingly, as if they belonged to someone else. Her head dropped another hand's width. Her tongue flickered out, tasting the air, and drew back in.

*Get up. Lift.* She lifted her head. It trembled on her neck, and the frost cracked in a fine web across her scales as she moved.

== strike take worn | has_b: feathers, wings
{B} had stopped trying to keep her feathers smooth. They stood out from her in ruffled tufts, out of place, broken here and there, and her wings hung half-open from her shoulders because folding them properly was more than she could manage.

The blow caught her on the chest.

It did not knock her down. She almost wished it had. Instead it went into her and stayed, and her whole body shook with it, every feather lifting and settling in a long shudder that ran out to the tips of her wings. She swayed. Her talons clenched on the ground to hold herself there.

Her beak opened on a gasp, and stayed open. The next breath came in with a whistle in it, and the one after that.

She drew her wings in round herself, slowly, as if for warmth, though she was not cold. Her eyes were half shut. Her crest, which had stood up proud all fight, lay flat and wet against her skull.

*Still standing.* She held onto that. Her legs trembled under her, and she spread her toes wider on the ground, and stayed.

== strike take light | has_b: horn
{B} took the blow on the side of her head and barely moved.

Her horn rang with it: a dull, hollow note that she felt in her teeth more than heard. That was all. She gave her head one short, hard shake, and the ringing went out of the horn and out of her skull, and she lowered her head again and looked at {A} along its length.

A small sting stayed where the blow had landed, just under the base of the horn. She noticed it, and filed it away, and let it go.

She was fresh and strong and angry, and a blow like that only told her where {A} was.

== strike take light | has_b: wings
It caught {B} on the trailing edge of her wing as she banked, and she felt it as a hard tug, as if something had grabbed a handful of her feathers and let go.

She tilted, corrected, and was level again in two beats. A few loose feathers spun away beneath her and drifted down. The wing stung, and she flexed it once, opening and closing it in the air, feeling the long feathers fan out and fold back, and it answered her: a little stiff at the edge, nothing worse.

*Close. Too close.* She climbed away, and her eyes stayed on {A} all the way up.

== strike take mid | has_b: fins | kind: electric
The charge hit {B} while she was still wet, and it went everywhere the water was.

Her fins snapped out stiff from her body, every spine standing rigid, and trembled there. Her jaw clamped. For a long heartbeat she could not breathe, could not move, could only feel the current running through her, along her sides, over the wet surface of her skin, into the soft places at the base of her fins where it stung like nettles.

Then it let go of her, and she gasped.

Her fins folded back against her body slowly, one at a time, the right one last. It stayed half-raised and twitching, and when she tried to tuck it in it only shivered. Her side ached in a long stripe where the current had run. She breathed hard through her mouth, her gills or nose or whatever she breathed with flaring, and shook herself, and water flew off her in a spray.

She was still strong. But the next time she would want to be dry, and she knew it.

== strike take mid | has_b: shell
The blow landed square on {B}'s shell with a hollow crack that echoed.

Her shell held. It always held. But the force of it went through the shell into her, and the shell, built to keep things out, kept it in instead: the shock rattled round inside, through her back, her shoulders, her ribs, and she felt it in her teeth.

She pulled her head in, half by instinct, and then forced it back out. Her breath came out in a short grunt. Her legs had braced wide on their own, claws digging into the ground.

There was a deep, dull ache along her back now where the shell met her body, and when she shifted her weight she felt the shell shift with her, sore in its moorings. She hissed through her teeth, and shook her head, and lifted it again to look at {A}.

== strike take hard | has_b: flame | kind: water
The water hit {B} full in the chest and she screamed.

It was not the force. It was what it did to her fire. The flame that burned along her back and at the tip of her tail shrank and guttered under the drenching, and she felt it go small and cold and frightened, the way a heart feels when it skips. Steam burst off her in a white cloud.

Her legs went weak under her. Not from pain; from fear. She had never felt her own fire so small.

She stood shaking in the steam with water running off her, her mouth open, panting, her eyes wide and white-ringed, and watched the flame on her tail flicker and catch and flicker again, and could not take her eyes off it. Her whole body was hunched round it, as if her body could keep it lit.

It caught. It steadied. It was half the size it had been.

She breathed out, a long, shaking breath, and her fire breathed with her.

== strike take hard | kind: psychic
Nothing touched {B}. That was the worst of it.

There was no blow, no contact, only a pressure that came out of nowhere and closed round her skull from the inside, and then squeezed. Her head snapped back as if she had been struck in the face. Her eyes squeezed shut, and behind them there was light, white and painful, and a high ringing that filled her ears and drowned out everything.

She grabbed at her head with both paws. There was nothing there to grab.

The pressure built, and held, and let go all at once, and she staggered under the release of it as if a weight had been lifted off her that she had been leaning on. Her eyes opened. The clearing swam, doubled, and slowly came back into one.

Her head throbbed. Not in one place: everywhere, all through it, a deep, sick ache that pulsed with her heart. She stood holding her skull, breathing in short gasps through her open mouth, and she could not tell, for a moment, which way was up.

== stagger take | kind: horn, charge
{A} came in head-down and the horn caught {B} in the shoulder, and the weight behind it was enormous.

She was lifted half off her feet. Her forelegs left the ground and she was driven backward, her hind legs scrabbling, two steps, three, her claws tearing up the earth as she tried to stop. She did not stop. The ground went out from under one foot and she lurched, nearly went down, threw her weight the other way and caught it.

She came to a halt crooked, half-turned, her shoulder screaming.

Her breath was coming in great heaving gasps. She looked down at her own shoulder, at the fur parted and pressed flat where the horn had struck, and back up at {A}, and for a moment she did not know which way to move.

Then her feet were under her again. She planted them, wide, and lowered her own head.

== stagger take | has_b: wings | kind: wind, flying
The gust caught {B} broadside with her wings half-open, and it took her like a leaf.

She went sideways across the ground in a stumbling, flapping rush, her talons skidding, her wings thrown wide and useless, every feather on her blown back the wrong way. She hit a rock with her hip and spun half round it, and her wings snapped shut against her body on their own, and she crouched there, flattened against the stone, while the wind roared past her.

When it dropped, she stayed crouched a moment longer.

Her feathers were all on end. Her crest was blown flat sideways. Her heart was hammering so hard she could feel it in her throat, and her breath was coming short and sharp through her open beak.

Slowly, carefully, as if she were testing ice, she straightened up. She shook herself, once, from head to tail, and the ruffled feathers lifted and fell back roughly into place, and she opened her wings to check them, and they opened.

== jolted take | kind: bite
{A}'s jaws were locked in the scruff of {B}'s neck, and {A} began to shake her.

Side to side, short and hard. There was nothing {B} could do; the grip was behind her head, out of reach of her own teeth or paws, and her body went wherever the jaws took it: left, her legs scrabbling; right, her shoulder slamming the ground; left again.

Every shake drove the teeth deeper. She felt each one as a fresh, wrenching burn at the back of her neck and a jolt that went all the way down her spine to her tail.

She tried to brace. She could not brace. Her eyes were shut and her mouth was open and a sound was coming out of her with every shake, a short, choked cry knocked out of her by the force of it, the same cry over and over.

When the shaking stopped, the jaws were still there. She hung in them, panting, her legs folded under her, feeling the teeth in her neck and the whole length of her body trembling.

== dwell
{B} stood very still and let the hurt find all of her.

It started at her ribs, where the last blow had gone in. A deep, hot throb, steady as a heartbeat. Then her shoulder answered it, the old bruise from earlier waking up. Then her jaw. Then the small, sharp sting at the back of her leg that she had forgotten until now. One by one, they all spoke.

She closed her eyes.

Her breathing was the loudest thing in the world. In, short and careful, catching at the top where the ribs would not let it go further. Out, long and shaking. In again. She pressed one paw flat to her side and felt her own ribs move under it, and felt the trembling there too, a fine, constant shiver she had not noticed starting and could not make stop.

*Just breathe. Just this one.*

A gasp got away from her when the ribs caught. Then another, smaller. She swallowed, and opened her eyes, and the light was too bright, and she let them close halfway.

Her legs were holding. She checked them, one at a time, the way you check that a door is locked. Still holding.

== dwell | has: coils, scales
{B} lay in a loose heap of coils, and every part of her that had been struck was talking at once.

There was no one place to hold. The hurt was spread down the length of her: a deep ache where the blow had folded her middle, a sharp sting near her tail, a throbbing at the back of her head. She drew herself in tighter, slowly, coil over coil, as if pulling the pain into one place where she could watch it, and every loop that moved woke something else.

She stopped moving. She lay with her head resting on her own coils, breathing.

Her body breathed in long, slow swells, and each one ran from her head down through her middle and out to her tail like a wave, and in the middle of each wave there was a hitch, a small catch where the hurt was. Her tongue flickered out, tasting the air. Her eyes stayed half shut.

A tremor ran down her length, scale by scale, and she let it run.

== watch | has_b: wings
{A} did not move. She stood a few lengths off and watched what the blow had done.

{B} had drawn her wings in round herself, not folded neatly, just pulled close, the way you pull a blanket round you. Her feathers were standing out all over her in ruffled tufts. Her head was low. Every few breaths, a shiver ran through her and lifted all her feathers at once, and they settled again slowly, never quite flat.

{A} watched the beak. It was open, and it stayed open, and the breath going in and out of it had a whistle in it.

She watched the wings. The left one was not quite closed. Its long feathers trembled at the tip, as if in a wind, though there was no wind.

*That one hurt. That one is going to stay hurt.*

She waited to see if {B} would lift her head. After a long moment, {B} did, slowly, and her eyes found {A}'s, and there was nothing in them yet but tiredness. Then, behind the tiredness, the fight came back.

== knockdown take | kind: rock
The boulder took {B} in the chest and the ground left her.

She did not fall so much as get thrown down: her feet went out from under her and her body slammed into the earth side-first, and the boulder rolled off her and away, and she lay where it had put her with the breath crushed out of her.

For a long moment she could not breathe at all. Her mouth opened. Nothing went in. Her ribs strained and strained against the ache in her chest and would not lift, and the edges of her sight went grey, and she clawed at the ground with one paw as if the air were somewhere in the dirt.

Then it came. One ragged, whooping gasp, and another, and her chest heaved.

She lay on her side with her eyes squeezed shut, panting, her legs drawn up a little, one paw pressed to her chest. Dust settled on her fur. Every breath scraped. She could feel exactly where the boulder had hit, a broad, deep ache in the middle of her chest, and around it a slow tremble spreading out through her whole body, to the tips of her paws.

She did not try to get up. Not yet.

# BUILD 177 (receiving side): more bodies and attacks for the takes. Each passage sells one blow: what it did to
# the body you can see, how the shock travelled, and the struggle after, sized to the blow, the part and how much
# of her is left. Conditions keep each one to fights where its bodies and attacks exist.

# strike take worn: a spent fighter, each with a different body and attack
== strike take worn | has_b: mane, arms | kind: punch, fighting
{B}'s mane had gone dark and heavy with sweat, and it hung in clumps about her neck instead of standing out round her face the way it had at the start.

The fist came up under her jaw.

It was not a hard punch. Fresh, she would have rolled her head with it and spat. Now her head snapped up and stayed up, chin to the sky, and the mane swung out and fell back against her neck with a soft slap she heard over the ringing. Her teeth had clicked together on the edge of her tongue. She tasted copper.

Her knees softened. She caught them, just, and stood there bent at the middle with her mouth open, dragging air in through it in long, rough pulls that rattled at the bottom.

*Not that. Anything but the jaw again.*

She lifted a hand to it and held it there, fingers spread along the bone, as if she could press the ache back in. The skin under her mane was hot. A tremor started in the hand and ran up her arm and into her shoulder, steady, small, and would not settle no matter how hard she pressed.

The dust of the clearing hung gold in the low light. She blinked it away. Her eyes kept wanting to close. She let them, for one breath, and opened them again on {A}.

== strike take worn | has_b: shell | kind: grass
The leaves came spinning in low, and {B} was too slow to pull her head in.

At the start she would have been gone into her shell before they left {A}. Now she only flinched, and the flinch came late, and three of the hard green edges skipped off the rim of her shell with dry ticking sounds and the fourth caught her across the side of the neck.

A thin line of sting opened there. That was all it was. It was enough.

Her whole body sank. The shell came down on her legs as they gave a little under it, and it seemed to weigh twice what it had weighed this morning, a great cold bowl pressing her toward the grass. A breath went out of her nose in a long, quavering hiss.

*Up. Head up. She can see you hiding.*

Her legs were trembling against the inside of the shell, and the trembling made the shell rock very slightly on the ground, a small helpless knock of rim on stone, again and again, that she could not stop.

# super effective take: an attack her body has no answer for

# coil take: wrapped and squeezed, a different body each time

Her wings had begun to tremble inside the coils, a fine buzzing shiver she could not stop and could not ease. She shut her eyes and listened to her own breath grow smaller.

== coil take | has_a: coils | has_b: shell
The coils could not crush {B}'s shell, and for a moment she thought that would be enough.

They went round it twice, sliding over the dome, and pulled tight, and the shell held, and inside it {B} breathed and waited. Then a third loop slid lower, down round the rim, and closed over the openings where her legs came out.

Now it was her legs. The coil pressed them back against the rim of her own shell, folded in at the knee, and she could not draw them in and could not push them out. The edge of the shell bit into the backs of them. Every time the coil tightened, the edge bit harder.

She hissed. It echoed in the hollow of the shell, loud and strange.

*It can't get in. It doesn't have to. It just has to wait.*

Her head was still out. She drew it in, and the coil shifted across the opening and stopped it halfway, so that her neck was held stretched between the rim and the scales, half in and half out.

She went still. Her breath sounded huge inside the shell, quick and ragged, and her legs had started to shake against the rim, a steady small knocking she could hear. The shell creaked in the coils' grip, a long low groan of pressure, and held, and held.

== coil take | has_a: coils | has_b: spikes
{A} wrapped her anyway, spikes and all, and {B} felt every one of her own spikes turned against her.

The coils came round her and pressed, and the spikes along her back and sides, which had always pointed out at the world, were pushed down flat, pressed back toward her own body. Their roots ached. Each one was a small hard point of pressure, and there were dozens of them, a whole field of little aches all down her back and flanks, pulsing.

The coil tightened on her breath out. Every spike pressed deeper.

A gasp got out of her, and then a groan, long and low and broken in the middle.

*Push them out. Make it hurt her too.*

She tried. She drew her body up and tensed, and the spikes rose, a little, against the scales. She felt them catch. Felt the coils flinch, just once. Then {A} squeezed harder, and they went flat again, harder than before, and the ache at their roots turned sharp.

She stopped trying. Her mouth hung open. She breathed in tiny quick sips through it, and each sip pressed her spikes against the coil and the coil pressed them back. A trembling had started under her skin, small and steady. Her eyes were shut tight. She could hear her own heart in her ears.

== coil take | has_a: coils | has_b: mane
The coil went round {B}'s neck and shoulders, and her mane went with it, dragged and gathered and crushed under the scales into a thick hot knot against her throat.

She could feel every hair of it pulling. The coil tightened and the knot of mane tightened with it, pulling at the skin of her neck from every direction at once, a sharp, prickling burn all round.

Then the second loop closed under her ribs, and she stopped thinking about her mane.

Her chest could not lift. She heaved against it and the heave went nowhere; the scales took it the way water takes a stone. The breath that came was half the size she needed. She took another. Smaller.

*Shoulders. Get one shoulder free.*

She twisted. Her mane slid an inch under the coil and pulled, and her eyes watered with it, and she twisted again and gained nothing.

Her mouth was open now, wide, and the sound of her breathing was loud and raw in it, a ragged rasp in and a groan out. Sweat had come up under her mane and the scales pressed it into her skin. Her legs were trembling where she stood inside the coil. She could not stop them, and the coil felt them shake and drew in a little more.

== coil take | has_a: coils | has_b: arms
The coil came round {B} high, over her arms, and pinned both of them to her sides before she could get them up.

That was the worst of it. She was used to having her hands. Now they hung trapped at her hips, pressed flat against her own legs, the fingers spread and useless. She could feel the scales against her palms, smooth and cool and shifting.

The coil tightened.

Her elbows were driven into her ribs. Her own arms became part of the squeeze, pushing in on her from both sides, and the breath went out of her in a choked grunt.

*Elbows out. Push. Push!*

She pushed. Her elbows moved a finger's width and the coil moved with them, and then took that finger's width back and more. Her shoulders ached from the strain. Her hands clenched into fists against her legs and stayed there, white-knuckled, shaking.

The breath came short. She dragged at it through her mouth and it came in thin and high and squeezed. Her face felt hot and full. Each heartbeat thudded in her temples, in her pinned hands, in the sore places where her elbows pressed her ribs.

She squeezed her eyes shut and kept her fists clenched, because if she let them go she did not know what she would do.

# grab take: seized by a part
== grab take | has_b: horn, forelegs | has_a: arms
{A}'s hands closed round {B}'s horn and wrenched.

{B}'s head went where the horn went. It had to: the horn was rooted in her skull, and when {A} hauled it sideways the whole of her head came with it, and then her neck, and then her shoulders, twisting after it. Her chin was dragged down nearly to her chest.

The ache came up from the root of the horn. A deep, dull, wrenching pain, low in her forehead, right where horn met bone, and it spread back through her skull like rings from a stone dropped in a pool.

She bellowed. It came out sideways, her mouth pulled half-open by the angle of her head.

*Pull back. Pull with your neck.*

She pulled. Her neck muscles bunched and strained and her front legs braced, and {A}'s grip held and turned the horn a little further, and the pain at the root flared white and her legs wobbled under her.

She stopped pulling. She stood bent where {A} held her, her head twisted down and sideways, panting through her open mouth, her eyes squeezed nearly shut. Her breath blew dust off the ground in front of her face. A tremor ran through her neck and into her shoulders, steady and fine, and every time it reached the root of the horn the ache throbbed with it.

== grab take | has_b: tails | kind: bite
{A}'s jaws closed on one of {B}'s tails, and {B} learned all at once how much of her was tied to it.

The bite itself was sharp, a ring of hot points through the fur near the tip. That she could have borne. But {A} pulled, and the tail pulled with her, and the pull went all the way down it to the root and into {B}'s back, a long stretching ache that ran along her spine.

Her other tails lashed. They flew out on their own, whipping round toward {A}'s face, and struck nothing; {A} had her head turned away, out of reach, the caught tail clamped tight in her teeth.

{B} yelped. It was a high, short, startled sound, ashamed of itself.

*Turn. Turn and face her. Don't pull against it.*

She turned. It eased the stretch at the root and nothing else. The teeth stayed in. She could feel them every time {A} breathed, the jaws tightening a little, letting go a little, the tail throbbing between them.

She stood half-turned, legs braced, panting. Her free tails had folded in tight against her flanks, trembling. The caught one twitched in {A}'s mouth, and every twitch hurt, and she could not stop it from twitching.

# throw take: lifted and thrown
== throw take | has_b: wings
{A} caught {B} by the base of one wing and turned, and {B} was off the ground.

She opened her wings. It was what wings were for. But she was going sideways and down, spinning, and the air came at them from the wrong side and crumpled them, and she flapped wildly and the flapping only turned her over. Earth. Sky. Earth.

She hit the ground on her side, wings flung out, and slid.

The landing drove up through her shoulder and the wing joint above it, a jarring, crushing ache. Dust rose round her. Feathers came loose and turned slowly in the air above her and settled on her like snow.

She lay where she had stopped, one wing folded under her and the other spread out across the ground, its long feathers bent and ruffled out of line. Her breath came in short, hoarse gasps. She could hear them rasping in her throat.

*Wings first. Check the wings.*

She drew the spread one in. It came, slowly, stiff at the shoulder, every feather trembling. The other one she freed by rolling off it, and it hurt at the joint, a hot ache where {A}'s grip had been and the ground had come after it.

She got her feet under her and stood, crouched, both wings half-open and shaking, and did not trust them yet to close.

== throw take | has_b: fins | has: fins
{A} dragged {B} up out of the pool and threw her, and she left the water in a long silver spray.

The air was wrong. All of it, instantly. Her fins had been spread to steer, and up here there was nothing to steer through, and they flapped and caught nothing. She turned over once in the air. She saw the water below her, and the bank, and then only the bank, coming up.

She landed on the dry rock with a flat, wet slap.

It went through her whole length. Her body bounced, once, and came down again, and lay there. Every fin she had stood straight out from her, stiff, quivering, and then slowly sank against her sides.

She could not breathe. That was the first thing. Out of the water, slammed, the breath knocked out of her: her mouth gaped and closed, gaped and closed, and nothing came. Her sides heaved.

*Water. Water. Behind you. Get back.*

She could hear it. It lapped against the rock a body's length away. She flapped against the stone, once, twice, a hard wet smack, and moved nowhere.

Then the breath came, ragged and rough. Her side ached where she had landed, a broad flat bruise, and the rock was hot and dry under her, drying her, and she lay there gasping, trembling all along her fins.

# clash take: two forces meet, and hers loses
== clash take | kind: fire
{B}'s fire met {A}'s fire in the middle of the air, and for a breath the two pushed against each other in a roaring wall of light.

She felt it come back to her down her own flame. It pressed against her open mouth, against her throat, a heat that was her own heat being pushed back down into her. She leaned into it. She put everything behind it.

{A}'s fire was bigger.

It rolled over hers. She saw her own flames bend back toward her, curling, and then the whole mass of it came through, both fires together, and broke over her face and chest.

She shut her eyes and mouth against it. Too late. She had breathed in the heat, and it scorched her throat on the way down. The skin of her face and chest drew tight in the heat. The smell of her own scorching filled her nose.

Her head jerked back. A cough tore out of her, hoarse and choking, and then another, smoke on her breath.

*It was mine. She sent back my own fire.*

The front of her face stung, a tight burning sting across the brow and mouth. Her eyes watered and would not stop. She stood with her head turned away, coughing, her chest heaving, and waited for the stinging to fade enough to look.

When it did, {A} was still there, the last of the flame fading from her open mouth.

== clash take | has_b: horn | kind: horn
{B} lowered her head and drove her horn at {A}'s, and {A} met her.

The two horns struck with a crack that rang off the rocks. For a heartbeat they held there, locked, both of them straining, their legs braced and digging. {B} felt the force come into her horn, and down through it into her skull, and down her neck into her shoulders, and the force kept coming.

Then {A} heaved, and {B}'s legs gave.

Her head was shoved up and back. Her front feet lifted, came down, skidded. She went back two steps, three, her horn knocked aside and her neck wrenched round with it, and she only stopped when her hindquarters bumped against a rock.

The ringing came first. A deep, hollow note, all through her skull, that she could not shake out of it.

Then the ache at the root of the horn, low in her forehead, thick and heavy and pulsing. Her eyes watered with it. She shut them.

*Too strong. She was too strong for me.*

She stood with her head low, horn tipped toward the ground, breathing hard through her nose in great snorting gusts. Her neck trembled with the strain it had taken. She swung her head once, slowly, side to side, and the ringing swung with it and did not leave.

== clash take | kind: psychic
{B} reached out with her mind, and {A}'s mind was there to meet it.

It was like pushing against a door that someone was pushing on the other side. She felt the weight of the other will pressing back at her, a cold steady pressure behind her eyes, and she pushed harder. Her head shook with the effort. Her eyes squeezed shut.

For a long moment, neither gave.

Then she felt her own will begin to bend. It went slowly at first, a softening, a slipping, and she tried to hold it and could not find anything to hold. Then it gave all at once, and {A}'s will came in through the gap.

It struck her behind the eyes. A burst of white light, a high thin ringing, and a pain like a fist closing inside her skull.

She cried out. Her head jerked back and her body rocked on its feet as if she had been slapped.

*Out. Get her out. Out of my head.*

The pressure withdrew, as quickly as it had come. It left the ache behind: deep, dull, throbbing behind her eyes in time with her heart. She stood with her eyes still squeezed shut, her mouth open, panting, and the world behind her eyelids was spinning very slowly. When she opened them, the light was too bright, and she let them close again, halfway.

# dive take: struck from above
== dive take | has_b: fins | has_a: beak | kind: beak
{B} was near the surface, her fins spread, gliding, and the light above her went dark.

Something hit the water above her with a sound like a stone thrown in. The surface broke. A beak came down through it, long and sharp and white with bubbles, and drove into her back between her fins.

The water slowed it. Not enough.

The blow pushed her down. She sank with it, her body bending round the place where the beak had struck, a hard deep jab that went into the muscle beside her spine and stayed there throbbing. Her fins snapped flat against her sides. Bubbles burst out of her mouth in a silver gust.

Then the beak was gone, pulled back up through the surface, and there was only a churning trail of foam above her where it had been.

*Up there. It came from up there. Where I can't see.*

She hung in the water, curled, her fins quivering. Her back ached in one sharp bright point and a wide dull ring round it. A thin dark wisp drifted up from the place and away in the current. Her breath came fast, too fast, and every breath pulled at the hurt in her back.

She dropped deeper, slowly, to where the water was dark, and looked up at the shining surface and waited for the shadow to come back.

== dive take | has_b: shell | kind: talon
The talons hit {B}'s shell from straight above, and the sound of it was enormous: a hard, clattering crash, like hail on a roof, but all at once.

== dive take | has_b: mane, forelegs | has_a: wings | kind: wing

# devastating take: a hard blow into a part already badly hurt
== devastating take | has_b: wings
The wing had been hanging wrong for a while. {B} had been holding it half-closed, away from her side, because closing it all the way pulled at the joint and opening it was worse.

{A}'s blow landed on that joint.

There was no sound from her at first. Her beak opened and nothing came out of it, and the wing flew wide on its own, every feather flung out stiff, and hung there shaking in the air. Then the sound came: a shriek so high and raw that it hurt her own ears, and went on until it ran out of breath and broke into a choked, gasping rattle.

== devastating take | has_b: tail | kind: bite
{B}'s tail had taken two bites already, and she had been carrying it low and stiff behind her, the fur along it matted and the muscle swollen hard under it.

{A}'s teeth found it a third time. In the same place.

== devastating take | has_b: scales | kind: claw
The scales on {B}'s flank had been scored three times in the same place, until they stood up ragged and loose and the skin under them was hot and swollen.

== devastating take | kind: electric
The bolt struck {B} on the shoulder, the shoulder that had been struck again and again all fight, until it was swollen hard and she could barely lift it.

It went in through the hurt, and found the hurt already open, waiting for it.

She did not feel the current spread through her the way it had before. It stayed. It sat in that shoulder and burned there, sizzling, a white-hot point that her whole body bent round. Her jaw locked open. Her legs locked straight. A strangled, buzzing sound was forced out of her through her teeth.

# breaking point: the part, or all of her, gives way
== breaking point | has_b: flame
{B}'s flame had been getting smaller for a while now. It flickered along her back in low, uneven tongues, and she could feel the cold coming in round the edges of it, a little more with each blow.

This one put it nearly out.

The blow drove into her side, and the flame on her back shrank down in a rush to a thin blue line, and the cold came in all at once. It came in under her skin, into her bones, a damp, shivering cold that she had never felt in all her life, and she cried out. Not at the blow. At the cold.

She sank down. Her legs folded under her and she crouched low against the ground, curled round herself, as small as she could make herself, as if she could hold the last of her heat in.

Slowly, slowly, the thin blue line along her back turned yellow at the edges. It was barely there. She held onto it.

== breaking point | has_b: antennae | kind: wind, flying
The wind took {B}'s antennae and bent them back, and that was the end of what she could take.

They had been sore all fight, battered by every gust, held low and close. Now the gale caught them and dragged them backward over her head, flat, bent at the base, and kept dragging. The pain came down from them into her skull in a single long white line.

*Can't. I can't. Not one more.*

She lay there with her face in the dirt, shaking all over, taking quick small breaths that caught and broke into sobs. Tears ran down into the dust. She did not lift her head.

== breaking point | has_b: feathers | has_a: beak | kind: beak
{A}'s beak had found {B}'s neck four times now, always the same side, always low, and the feathers there were gone in a ragged patch and the skin under them was dark and swollen.

# sustain take: an attack that keeps coming, held on her
== sustain take | has_b: feathers, beak, wings | kind: fire
The fire did not stop when it reached {B}. It kept pouring.

The first of it was only heat, a hard dry blast against her breast that made her squint. Then it went on, and the heat built, and her feathers began to go. She felt them curl at the tips, all across her front, tightening, and smelt them: a bitter, choking reek that filled her nostrils.

She turned her shoulder into it. The fire followed her round.

It found the soft down under the outer feathers and the down shrank away from it, and then there was only skin, and the skin began to sting, then burn, then blaze, a wide flat glow of pain across her breast and shoulder that went on growing as long as the fire went on.

*Stop. Stop. When does it stop?*

Her beak was open. She was panting into the heat and every breath scorched. A long, ragged cry came up out of her and the flames swallowed it.

Then, at last, it stopped.

She stood in the drifting smoke with her front feathers singed short and curled, her breast throbbing in a hot, even wash of pain. Her wings had come up round her on their own, half-shielding her, and they were trembling. She did not lower them. == sustain take | has_b: fins | kind: ice
The cold came at {B} in a steady glittering stream and did not let up, and she was out of the water with nowhere to hide from it.

It caught her fins first. They were wet, and the wet froze at once, a thin white crust along every edge, and the fins stiffened under it. She tried to fold them in. They would not fold. They stood out from her body rigid and shining, and the cold crept inward along the spines toward her skin.

== sustain take | has_b: ruff | kind: grass
The vines had her round the middle, and they were drinking from her.

{B} felt it start as a faint pulling under her skin where the green coils touched, as if something were drawing on her the way roots draw on wet soil. It did not hurt at first. It only felt wrong. Then it went on, and on, and her legs began to feel hollow.

She strained against the vines. They did not give. The pulling went on through her straining, steady and patient, and she felt her strength go out of her into them, a little more with every heartbeat.

Her ruff, which had been standing out thick and proud round her neck, began to sag. She felt it settle against her shoulders, heavy and limp.

*It's taking it. It's taking all of it.*

== sustain take | has_b: wings, beak | kind: wind, flying
The gale came at {B} head-on and kept coming, and she could do nothing but hold herself down against it.

She had folded her wings tight at the first gust. It did not matter. The wind found every gap. It got under the edges of her feathers and pried at them, lifting them, peeling them back from her body one by one, until her whole back was a ruffled mass of feathers turned the wrong way.

It pulled at her wings. She clamped them to her sides with every muscle she had, and the muscles began to burn, and the wind pulled harder.

It ended.

The silence was sudden. She stayed crouched, too afraid to lift her head. Her wing muscles were trembling uncontrollably now, a deep, exhausted quiver, and an ache ran from the joints of both wings into her back. Her breath whistled in and out through her beak. Every feather on her body stood on end, and slowly, slowly, they began to settle.

# pin start take: brought down and held
== pin start take | has_b: wings
{B} hit the ground on her back with her wings spread, and before she could fold them {A} was on her.

A weight came down on each wing. Not on her body: on the wings, pressing them flat into the dirt on either side of her, wide open, the long feathers splayed out under {A}'s weight. She felt the joints stretch.

*No. Not like this. Not open.*

She heaved her body up. It rose a hand's width and her wings did not rise with it, and the pull at the joints where they met her shoulders stopped her dead. She dropped back with a short, sharp cry.

Then the rest of {A}'s weight settled, onto her breast, and her breath went out of her and came back thin.

She lay spread under {A}, her wings pinned wide, every feather on them trembling against the dirt. She could feel each one. She could feel the grit under them, and the weight on top, and the ache building at both shoulders, slow and dragging.

Her beak gaped. Her breast rose under {A}'s weight in short, quick heaves, each one shallower than she wanted. Her eyes went from one pinned wing to the other and back to {A}'s face, very close above her.

*Wait. She has to move sometime.*

She made herself go still, and felt her wings shaking against the ground anyway.

== pin start take | has_b: coils | has_a: forelegs
{A} came down on {B}'s neck with both forelegs, just behind the head, and pressed it to the stone.

The rest of {B} exploded. Her coils whipped and thrashed, a heavy writhing surge that flung dust and grit across the ground, loop after loop flailing, slamming, twisting. Her tail lashed at {A}'s flank and struck it, and struck it again.

It did not matter. The head was held. Everything that was {B} began there, and {A} had it, and all that thrashing behind went nowhere.

*Head. Get the head out. Pull!*

She pulled. Her whole body hauled back against the pin, and the forelegs on her neck pressed harder, and the stone ground into her jaw. Her mouth was held half-open against the rock. Her tongue flickered out and tasted dust.

The thrashing slowed. It had to. She was spending everything and gaining nothing, and her body knew it before she did. The loops slumped and lay heaving on the ground behind her, the scales along them rising and falling with each breath.

She lay with her head pinned and her eyes fixed on the foreleg pressing her neck, close, enormous. Her neck ached where the weight sat on it, and every breath she took had to squeeze past it, short and hissing. A long tremor ran down her coils to the tip of her tail.

# strike take mid / hard / light: a kick, a claw at the crest, a fairy sting on a fresh body
== strike take mid | has_b: forelegs | kind: fighting
{A} spun and kicked, and the heel took {B} in the ribs just behind her foreleg.

It landed with a deep, flat thump, like a fist on a drum. The fur over her ribs dented in round it and sprang back out. Her breath left her through her nose in one hard snort, and the foreleg on that side buckled at the elbow, dipping her shoulder toward the ground before she locked it straight again.

*Behind the leg. Where I can't cover it.*

The ache came in after, slow and thick. It spread out from the place under her fur and wrapped round her side and settled into the ribs themselves, so that the next breath stopped halfway and would not come further. She took a smaller one. Then another.

Her side twitched, a little jump of the skin where the heel had struck, and then again. She leaned her weight off it, onto the other foreleg, and held her head low, mouth open, panting softly.

The wind came across the open slope and flattened the grass round her feet. She stayed braced, the hurt side drawn in a little, and watched {A} set her feet for the next one.

== strike take hard | has_b: crest | kind: claw
{A}'s claws came across the top of {B}'s head and caught her crest, and raked through it.

{B} had been carrying the crest high all fight, proud and stiff, and already it had taken a blow there, and the base of it was tender and swollen. Now the claws dragged across that tenderness and the crest was wrenched over to one side with them and let go.

The pain went down through her skull like a nail. She cried out, short and sharp, and her head jerked down, chin tucked, as if she could pull the top of her skull out of reach.

The crest did not spring back. It lay over to one side, flattened, three thin lines scored across its base, and a warm thread ran down from one of them over her brow and into the corner of her eye.

*My crest. She went for my crest.*

She blinked the warm away. Her eyes were watering, both of them, and her head throbbed at the crown in great slow beats that she could feel behind her eyes. Her mouth hung open. Each breath came in short and caught, and came out as a low, shaking groan.

She tried to lift the crest. It rose a little, quivering, and the ache at its base flared so bright she let it fall again, and stood with her head bowed under it, trembling.

== strike take light | kind: fairy
The light burst against {B}'s cheek in a spray of pink sparks, bright and sudden, and stung like a slap.

She blinked. That was the worst of it: for a breath the whole side of the meadow was spotted with dancing afterglow, and she had to turn her head and squint to see past it. Her cheek tingled where it had landed, a warm, prickling buzz that faded as she noticed it.

She shook her head once, hard, and the spots cleared. A few last sparks drifted down past her nose and winked out in the clover at her feet, and somewhere off in the long grass a cricket, startled quiet by the flash, began again.

*That's all? Sparkles?*

Her breath had not even quickened. She rolled her jaw, and the last of the prickle went out of it, and she set herself and looked at {A} with clear eyes, fresh and steady, and waited for something with weight in it.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): FALL. {A} puts {B} down; the fall told through {B}'s body and {A}'s eyes on it.

== fall | has_b: forelegs | kind: charge
{A} lowered her head and came across the open ground in a straight line, and {B} saw it too late to do anything but brace.

The bracing was the mistake. {B} had set her forelegs stiff and square, and {A} struck her right between them, chest to chest, with all her running weight. The forelegs did not bend. They had nowhere to go but out, and they went, both at once, sliding apart in the dust like the legs of a dropped table.

Her chest came down first. It hit the ground with a flat, heavy thud that drove a grunt out of her, and her chin followed it and bounced, and dust puffed up round her face. Her hind end stayed up a moment longer, ridiculous, the hind legs still standing, and then they folded too and she was down on her belly, sprawled.

{A} pulled up a length past her and turned, sides heaving.

*Down. Finally down.*

{B} lay with her forelegs splayed out in front of her and her jaw on the earth. Her ribs pumped against the ground. She tried to draw the forelegs back under her and the right one came but the left only dragged, scraping a short furrow in the dirt, and stopped. Her eyes squeezed shut. A low groan came out of her, half breath.

== fall | has_b: wings | kind: rock
{B} had been hanging out of reach all fight, and {A} had stopped trying to reach her. She threw the stone instead.

It went up heavy and turning, and {B} saw it and tipped to slide under it, and was not quite quick enough. It caught her on the shoulder at the root of the wing.

The wing stopped working. Not hurt, not yet; simply stopped, folded half shut by the blow, and the other wing went on beating alone and turned her over in the air. {A} watched her fall. It was not a dive. It was a tumble, crooked and helpless, her good wing flailing to catch the air and catching only enough to slow her a little, feathers flaring and spilling.

She hit the ground on her side. The sound reached {A} a heartbeat after the sight: a soft, heavy slap, and a burst of loose feathers that went up and drifted down over her.

{B} lay with the struck wing crumpled under her and the free one spread out flat on the earth, its long feathers trembling. Her beak was open. Her breast heaved. When she tried to lift her head it came up only a little, wobbling, and sank.

*Stay down there. You're mine down there.*

== fall | has_b: coils | kind: tail
{B} held her head high above her coils, swaying, the way she had held it all fight, and {A} meant to bring it down.

She swung her tail low and flat across the ground, under the raised head, into the thickest part of the coils where the body bent to lift itself. It landed with a deep, dull smack. {A} felt the whole long body take it.

{B} lay spread out in a loose, crooked line. Her length rippled where the tail had struck, a twitch that ran a little way along her scales and died. Her tongue flicked out, touched the dirt, drew back.

*All that height, and now look at you.*

{B} gathered herself. A loop drew in, slow, then another. Her head lifted a hand's width off the ground and hung there, trembling, before it found the strength to go higher.

== fall | has_a: arms | has_b: legs | kind: punch
{A} stepped inside the reach and put her fist into {B}'s middle, low, just under the ribs, and drove up.

She felt it go in. She felt the breath leave {B} against her knuckles, a hot rush of it, and felt the body fold forward over her arm, heavy suddenly, as if all its weight had arrived at once. {A} pulled her arm back and stepped clear.

{B}'s legs went. They did not buckle so much as forget themselves: the knees softened, and she sat down hard on the ground with a jolt that shook her whole frame and snapped her head forward.

She stayed there, sitting, folded over her own middle. Her arms had come round it on their own. Her mouth hung open and nothing was going in, and her eyes were wide and blank and looking at the ground between her feet.

Then the breath came back to {B}, all at once, a long, ragged, whooping pull, and another, and with it a low moan that she pressed her lips shut on too late. Her shoulders shook. She did not try to stand. Not yet.

== fall | has_b: shell | kind: ground
{A} struck the earth with all her weight, and the ground under {B} heaved.

It rose under her like a wave and broke, and {B}, heavy and low and wide, rode up on it with her legs scrabbling for purchase. She was built to stand on flat earth. She was not built for this. The ground tilted, and her weight went with it, and the edge of her shell dipped, caught, and she went over.

She landed on her back with a deep, hollow knock that {A} felt through her own feet.

And there she stayed. Her shell rocked once, twice, settling into the broken earth. Her legs worked at the air, reaching, slow, finding nothing. Her head stretched out from the front of the shell and twisted, straining, and her mouth was open and her breath came out of her in short, harsh huffs.

{A} came round her at a walk.

The underside was pale and bare and plain to see. {B} knew it. Her head swung toward {A}, and a hiss came out of her, thin with effort, and her legs reached harder for the ground, and the shell only rocked.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): TUMBLE. {A}'s blow sends {B} rolling over the ground; the body hits, turns, hits again.

== tumble | has_b: wings, feathers | kind: water
{A} gathered the water in her and let it go in one heavy rope, straight at the middle of {B}'s body, and {B}, caught on the ground with her wings half open, took it full.

It did not knock her down. It rolled her. Her open wings caught the force like sails and she went over sideways, wing, back, other wing, a wet heavy tumble across the stones. Each turn slapped a wing against the ground. Feathers came loose and stuck to the rock. She rolled until the slope ran out and fetched up against a tussock, belly up, with her wings spread crooked on either side of her.

{A} let the water run out of her and stood breathing.

{B} was soaked to the skin. Her feathers had gone dark and flat and clung to her, so that she looked half her size, all neck and bone. She dragged her wings in and they came slowly, heavy with water, dripping. Her breast heaved.

{B} got her head up. Water ran off her beak. She shook herself, a hard full-body shake that threw spray in a ring, and her feathers lifted, sodden, and fell back flat, and she made a small, furious, shivering sound.

== tumble | has_b: shell | kind: charge

{B} shook her head, hard. Her legs came out and spread wide and pressed flat into the ground as if the ground might start turning again. A long breath went out of her, shaking.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): SUSTAIN ACT. {A} keeps an attack on {B} and feels it working, a breath at a time.

== sustain act | has_a: flame | kind: fire
{A} opened her mouth and did not close it again.

The fire came out of her in a steady roaring stream, and she held it on {B} the way you hold a hand over a candle, patient, unmoving. Her own flame flared high behind her with the effort, and she felt the heat come back off the target onto her face and narrowed her eyes against it and kept pouring.

{B} turned her shoulder into it. It did not help. {A} moved the stream with her, a small turn of the head, and laid it along the shoulder and down the flank, and watched the fur there darken and curl and smoke.

*Stay in it. Stay in it a little longer.*

{B} tried to back out of the fire and the fire followed. Her head went down and to the side, eyes screwed shut, mouth open on a sound {A} could not hear over the roar. Her whole body hunched round the burning side, smaller and smaller.

{A} ran out of breath before she ran out of will. The stream thinned, flickered, and died, and she stood with smoke curling out of her jaws, chest heaving.

{B} stood wreathed in her own smoke, shaking, her singed flank twitching in little hard jumps under the scorched fur, and she did not lift her head.

== sustain act | kind: ice
The cold came out of {A} in a pale hissing beam, and she kept it there.

She aimed it at {B}'s legs first, low, where the ground was, and held. Frost bloomed on the earth round {B}'s feet and climbed. {B} jerked and tried to lift one foot free and it came up slow, rimed white, heavy, and went down again.

{A} drew the beam up her body. Slowly. A hand's width at a time. Frost followed it up the legs and over the flank, a white crust that crackled when {B} moved, and {B} moved less and less. Her breath smoked. Her jaw had begun to clatter, teeth against teeth, a small fast sound under the hiss of the cold.

*Slower. Make her feel all of it.*

{A}'s own chest ached with the cold going out of it. Her eyes stung. She held on.

When she let it go at last, {B} was white from the feet to the shoulders and shaking so hard the frost on her sifted down in a fine powder. She did not try to step. She stood hunched, every muscle drawn tight against the chill, and her breath came out of her in short white puffs, quick and shallow, and her eyes were half closed against the glare of her own frozen side.

== sustain act | kind: bite
{A} had her teeth in {B}'s shoulder, and she was not letting go.

She did not shake. Shaking would have loosened the grip. She only bore down, steadily, setting her jaw a little deeper every time {B} pulled, and felt the muscle under her teeth bunch and twist and fail to get away.

{B} pulled hard. {A} went with the pull just enough to keep her footing and bit down on the end of it.

*There. Feel that. Keep feeling it.*

A sound came out of {B} that was halfway between a snarl and a whine. Her free side thrashed. Then it stopped, and {A} felt the change through her teeth: the shoulder going tight and trembling, the body round it holding still on purpose, because every movement made it worse.

{A} breathed through her nose, slow, her jaw aching, the taste of fur and a thin salt sting on her tongue. She could hear {B}'s breath close to her ear, ragged, catching each time {A}'s jaw shifted. She could feel the trembling travel out from the bite into the rest of the body.

She held on until her own jaw shook. Then she let go all at once and stepped back.

{B} stayed hunched, the bitten shoulder drawn in high and tight, a row of small red marks showing through the wet, flattened fur.

== sustain act | kind: electric
{A} put the current into {B} and kept it flowing.

The first instant was the bright one: the snap, the light, {B}'s body jerking rigid. {A} had felt that before. What she wanted now was what came after, if she held on. She held on.

The current hummed between them. {B} stood locked inside it, every muscle fighting every other, her legs braced and shaking, her head pulled back at a stiff angle, her jaws clamped. She could not scream. {A} could see her trying: the throat working, the chest heaving and getting nothing.

*Not yet. Not yet.*

The air smelled scorched. The hair or feather or scale on {B}'s back stood up and crackled with tiny blue sparks. {A} felt the drain of it in her own body, a hollow pull in her chest as the charge went out of her, and her legs began to shake too.

She cut it off.

{B} folded at once, not down, but in, sagging over her own middle, gasping like something pulled out of water. Small twitches ran over her: her shoulder, her flank, a jump at the corner of her mouth. She could not stop them. She stood there twitching and breathing and blinking, and {A} stood breathing too, and neither of them moved for a long moment.

== sustain act | kind: psychic
{A} found the inside of {B}'s head and pressed, and did not stop pressing.

There was nothing to see between them. That was the strange part. {A} only stood with her eyes fixed and her whole body gone very still, and across the open ground {B} began, slowly, to bend.

It started at the neck. {B}'s head tipped as if something heavy had been set on top of it. Then her shoulders came down to meet it. {A} leaned into the pressure, quietly, the way you lean on a door that is starting to give, and {B}'s eyes squeezed shut and her face tightened all over.

*I can feel you pushing back. Push harder. It won't help.*

{B} did push. {A} felt it, a flutter of resistance in the dark space between them, and pressed through it, and it went soft. {B} swayed on the spot. Her breath was coming in thin gasps through her teeth. She shook her head, hard, once, as if she could throw the weight off, and it only sank deeper.

{A}'s own temples had begun to throb. A thin ache, behind the eyes. She let go.

{B} jerked upright like a rope snapping, swayed, and stood blinking with her head lowered and her eyes unfocused, breathing as if she had run a long way.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): COIL. {A} winds round {B} and squeezes; {A} feels the struggle and the breath inside her hold.

== coil | has_a: coils | has_b: wings
{A} had her wrapped before she could open her wings, and that was the whole of it.

The first loop went round {B}'s body over both wings, pinning them to her sides, and {A} felt the feathers flatten under her scales and the long quills bend. The second loop went lower. Then she tightened.

{B} fought it with the only thing she had, which was her wings, and her wings could not move. {A} felt them try. A shove from inside, from both sides at once, the strong shoulders straining against the coils, the feathers rasping on her scales. She let the shove come and took the room it gave up when it stopped.

*Every time you push, you lose a little more.*

{B}'s beak was open. {A} watched it from above, her own head swaying a little, and heard the breath going in and out of it get smaller, whistling at the top. A quill had come loose and stuck out between two loops, trembling.

{A} tightened again.

The body inside her coils gave a long shudder that she felt from end to end, and then the struggling went faint and slow, a small push and a pause, a smaller push. {B}'s eyes had closed. Her head drooped against the top loop, and her breath fluttered there, quick and thin, against {A}'s scales.

== coil | has_a: coils | has_b: spikes
{A} had to be careful where she held, and she was.

{B}'s back bristled with spikes, and {A} did not wrap there. She went low instead, round the softer middle beneath them, and drew the loop in slowly, feeling for the gaps, and every time a spike pressed into her side she shifted a little and let it find the space between two scales.

{B} bucked. The spikes along her back stood up stiff and rattled, and one of them raked across {A}'s upper coil and left a thin sting there. {A} hissed and did not let go. She drew the second loop tighter and pinned the body lower, where the spikes could not reach to turn on her.

*Prickly little thing. It won't save you.*

She felt the struggle shift. {B} stopped trying to stab her and started trying to breathe. Under {A}'s coils the middle heaved, short and hard, fighting for room, and each heave got a little less. {B}'s head hung forward. Her mouth gaped. The spikes along her back trembled and began, slowly, to lie down.

{A} held it. Her own side stung where the spike had scraped her, a small bright line, and she ignored it and listened to {B}'s breath going thin.

== coil | has_a: tail | has_b: arms
{A} got her tail round {B}'s chest from behind, under the arms, and pulled it tight.

It was not built for this the way a serpent's body is. It was strong, though, and long enough to go round and lock over itself, and once it was locked {A} leaned back against it and let her weight do the squeezing.

{B}'s arms came up at once and clawed at the tail. {A} felt fingers dig in, felt them pull, felt the grip slip on her fur. She tightened under them. The tail bit in over {B}'s ribs and {B}'s breath went out of her in a cough.

{A} could feel the ribs working against her tail, every breath a hard shove outward that she met and held. The shoves came fast at first. Then slower. {B}'s hands, still locked on the tail, were not pulling any more. They were only holding on, and shaking.

{A}'s own tail burned with it, a deep ache all along it from root to tip. She set her teeth and kept the lock. {B} had sagged against her, head lolling back on {A}'s shoulder, mouth open, and {A} could hear the breath going in and out of her in small, thin, rasping sips.

== coil | kind: grass

{A} drew them up. They climbed, winding and thickening, over {B}'s hips, round her middle, across her chest, and {A} felt every coil of them as if it were part of her own body: the warmth under them, the pulse, the push of the ribs.

{B} strained in the green coils, every muscle standing out under her hide, her head thrown back. == coil | has_a: coils | has_b: forelegs
{B} was too big to wrap all at once, so {A} took her a piece at a time.

She went round the forelegs first, low, binding them together so they could not brace, and {B} pitched forward onto her chest with a grunt. Then {A} slid up over her shoulders and round her ribs, one loop, a second, cool scales over warm fur, and settled her weight and closed.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): OFF TARGET. {A} aims at one place and the blow lands somewhere else, still telling.
