== off target | has_a: horn | kind: horn
{A} put her head down and went for the belly, where the horn would land hardest and do the most.

{B} turned. Not much. A half-twist of the body, a step that was barely a step, but it was enough: the soft underside swung away and the shoulder swung in to take its place, and {A}'s horn met hard muscle instead of soft.

It still landed. {A} felt the jar of it run up her horn into her skull and down her neck. {B} rocked on the spot and a grunt burst out of her, and her shoulder dipped under the blow and came back up slow.

*The shoulder. I wanted the belly.*

{A} drew back, shaking her head to clear the ringing out of her horn. {B} was holding the shoulder tight to her body, and the muscle under the skin there was twitching in small jumps. Her eyes were watering. But her belly was still covered, and she knew it, and she kept it turned away.

== off target | kind: beam
{A} aimed the beam at {B}'s head and let it go.

It went straight. It was meant to. But {B} turned her face from it at the last instant, not dodging, only flinching, the way anything flinches from light, and the beam skimmed past her cheek and took her on the side of the neck instead.

{A} saw it land wrong and felt a flicker of something like disappointment and then forgot it, because it was still landing. {B}'s head snapped round. A ragged cry broke out of her and stopped short. {B} lifted her head slowly. She held it a little crooked, the struck side tucked down and away, and when she swallowed {A} saw her face tighten.

== off target | has_b: wings | kind: claw
{A} raked for the body, under the wing where it joined, and {B} lifted the wing to cover it.

# NEW SAMPLES: ESCAPE AFTERMATH. {A} has just broken free; her body still carries the hold.

== escape aftermath | has_a: wings
{A} was free, and her wings did not believe it.

They had been crushed flat under {B}'s weight for so long that when she tried to open them they came out stiff and crooked, like something unfolded from a box. The right one opened halfway and stuck. A deep, dragging ache ran from its joint across her back to the other shoulder, and she sucked air through her beak and held still until it eased.

Her feathers were a ruin. Bent the wrong way in a broad flat patch where {B} had pressed on them, crushed and ruffled everywhere else. She gave herself a shake, and they lifted, and most of them would not lie down again.

*Up. Get up off the ground. Anywhere but under her.*

She beat once. The wings worked, sort of; the right one a beat behind the left, the joint grinding. It lifted her a little and set her down again on her feet, swaying.

Her breath came quick and short. Her chest still felt the weight on it, a pressure that was not there, and she had to make herself breathe deeper, past it. Each deep breath caught at the bottom. She stood with her wings half spread and trembling, too sore to fold, and kept her eyes on {B}.

== escape aftermath | has_a: coils
{A} slid out from under {B}'s weight and kept sliding until there was ground between them, and only then did she let herself feel it.

Every part of her that had been pressed down was waking up. A long, flat ache ran down the middle of her where the weight had lain across her length, and the coils there were slow to answer. She drew herself together and they came grudgingly, the bruised stretch dragging a little behind the rest, so that she gathered in a crooked heap instead of a clean spiral.

She lifted her head. It wobbled. She lowered it a little and let it hang there, swaying, while she breathed.

Her body breathed in long slow swells, and in the middle of each one there was a hitch, a small catch where the bruising was. Her tongue flickered. Her scales were scuffed dull along one side, dust ground into them.

*Out. You're out. Don't let her near you again.*

A tremor went down the length of her from head to tail, and she let it go, and tightened her coils as well as she could, and lifted her head a little higher, and fixed {B} with a flat, unblinking stare.

== escape aftermath
{A} was out of the hold and on her feet, and she could not stop touching her throat.

{B} had held her there a long time. Long enough that the grip had become the whole world, a band of pressure under the jaw and every breath squeezed small to get past it. Now nothing was there, and her throat did not know it. It still felt held. She swallowed and it hurt, a raw scraping ache all down the inside, and she coughed, and that hurt worse.

She breathed. Deep, on purpose, and the air went down cool and rough and burned, and she did it again, and again, because she could.

Her legs were not steady. She stood with them braced wide and felt them shaking under her, a fine quick tremble she could not stop. Her head felt light. The ground tilted, just slightly, and came back.

*Breathe. Breathe. Then fight.*

She coughed again, doubled a little with it, and straightened slowly. Her neck was stiff from being held at an angle, and when she turned her head to find {B} the muscles down the side of it pulled tight and stayed tight.

== escape aftermath | has_a: fins
{A} was free and back on her own, and her fins were the first thing that told her what it had cost.

They had been crushed against her sides the whole time {B} held her, and now when she tried to open them they would not lie right. The left one opened with a sharp sting at its root and stood out bent, a crease across the middle of it where it had been folded under her. The right one opened and trembled and folded back down on its own.

She held still and let them settle. Every small movement of them sent a sharp ache along her side.

Her skin was dry where it had been pressed against the ground, rough and tight and sore, and she wanted water badly. She breathed through her mouth, quick, shallow pants that would not go deeper.

*Get them working. You'll need them.*

She opened the left fin again, slowly, wincing, and spread it as far as it would go, and the crease in it straightened a little. Her eyes watered. She closed them a moment, breathing, and then opened them and turned until {B} was in front of her again.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): DEVASTATING. {A} lands the blow of the fight on a part already hurt; it sells all the way through {B}.

== devastating | has_b: legs | kind: bite
{B} had been favouring the leg for a while. {A} had watched her do it: the weight lifted off it, the careful steps, the way she kept that side turned away. Now {B} stumbled on rough ground and the leg came down to catch her, and it was right there.

{A} took it.

Her jaws closed on it above the knee, where the earlier bite still showed, and she bit with everything. She felt the hurt muscle clench under her teeth and the whole leg go stiff.

{B} screamed. It was not a cry or a yelp. It was a scream, high and cracked and long, and it went on after {A} let go. The leg folded. {B} went down on that side, the leg drawn up tight against her body, trembling so hard that {A} could see the trembling from where she stood.

*That was the one. That was the one that mattered.*

{B} lay curled round her leg, breath sobbing in and out of her, short and ragged. Her eyes were squeezed shut and tears ran from the corners of them. Every time the leg twitched, she whimpered. She did not try to stand. She did not look as if she could remember how.

{A} stood over her with her jaw aching and the taste of the bite in her mouth, and her own breath coming hard, and waited.

== devastating | has_b: wings | kind: ice
The wing had been hanging wrong since the last exchange, and {B} could not hide it. Every time she banked, it dragged. Every time she landed, it came in a beat late and folded crooked.

{A} waited until she landed.

The cold went out of her straight at the bad wing, and {B} saw it and could not lift the wing fast enough to turn it away, and it struck full on the joint where the hurt already was.

{A} watched it freeze. Frost spread over the feathers in a white rush, out along the long flight feathers to the tips, and the wing went stiff, spread half open and locked there. {B}'s whole body jerked. A shriek tore out of her beak, raw and shuddering, and went on and on.

She could not fold it. She tried, and the frozen joint would not bend, and the trying made her shriek again, smaller, and then stop, and stand with her beak open and her body shaking, and the white wing standing out from her side like something that belonged to someone else.

*She won't fly on that. Not today.*

{B}'s legs were trembling. Her head sank. Her breath came in short, whistling gasps, each one catching. When the frost began to melt and drip, she whimpered at every drop.

== devastating | has_a: arms | kind: punch
{A} had hit those ribs twice already. She had seen what it did: the flinch, the breath cut short, the way {B} curled round that side now without thinking. And now {B} reached high, too high, and the side came open under her arm.

{A} stepped in and drove her fist into it with her whole body behind it.

She felt the blow land on the hurt place exactly. She felt the ribs under it, already sore, take everything she had. {B}'s breath came out of her in a noise that was not a word or a cry but a sort of strangled bark, and her arm came down over the side, too late, and her whole body bent round it.

When the breath finally came it came as a sob. {B}'s eyes were squeezed shut. She swayed on the spot, and a long, low moan leaked out between her teeth, and her free hand groped at the air for something to hold.

== devastating | kind: dragon
{A} had been building it all fight, and when she let it out it came out of her like a storm.

The dragon-fire roared across the space between them, blue and violet, wild, and it found {B} on the side she had been guarding, the side that had already taken the worst of the fight. {B} saw it coming. She turned to put her shoulder in the way. It was not enough.

# NEW SAMPLES: COLLAPSE. {B} goes down on her own, the body giving out; {A} sees it happen.

== collapse | has_b: wings, feathers
{B} had landed to rest, and {A} let her. It was the first time in a long while that {B} had come down to the ground and stayed there.

{A} watched her stand. Her wings hung half open at her sides because folding them took more than she had. Her feathers stood up in ruffled patches. Her head was low and her beak was open and her breath came in and out of it in quick, light pants, with a small rasp at the top of each one.

{B}'s legs gave out without a sound. She went down onto her breast, not hard, almost gently, and her wings slid open across the ground on either side of her like a dropped cloak. Her head sank until her beak rested in the dirt.

She did not try to rise. Her eyes were half shut. Her breast rose and fell under the ruined feathers, quick and shallow, and a long faint shiver ran out along both wings to their tips and came back. One wing twitched, as if to lift. It stayed where it was.

== collapse | has_b: coils
{A} had pulled back to breathe, and in the stillness she watched {B} fall without being touched.

It was slow. That was what she would have said about it, if she had had anyone to say it to. {B} had been holding her head high above her coils, swaying a little, watching. Then the swaying grew wider. Her head went too far one way, came back, went too far the other. The loops beneath her shifted and settled and could not seem to hold their shape.

She could not. The neck sank. It did not drop; it bent, slowly, like a stalk losing its water, until the head lay on top of the coils, and then the coils themselves slumped outward, sliding apart, until {B} was not a spiral any more but a long loose curve on the ground.

== collapse | has_b: forelegs
{A} stood off and watched {B} lose her footing to nothing at all.

There was no blow. {B} had only been standing, head low, sides heaving, gathering herself the way she had gathered herself time after time already. But her hind legs had begun to tremble, and {A} saw them: a quick fine shaking, there in the thick muscle of the thighs, that would not stop.

{A} did not know which of them she was talking to.

Her flanks worked hard and fast. Her eyes had drifted half closed. A low sound came out of her on every breath out, not quite a groan, more a sigh with a catch in it. The set had gone out of her neck. She did not look at {A}. She did not look at anything.

== collapse | has_b: horn
{A} watched the horn, because the horn told her everything.

All fight it had been up, high and steady, pointed at her. {B} was not hurt any worse than she had been a moment ago. {A} had not touched her. But something was running out.

# NEW SAMPLES: OPENING. The place, then the two of them arriving and taking each other's measure.

== opening | has_a: wings | has_b: coils
The ridge was bare rock, scoured clean by wind, and the wind was still at it. It came up the slope in long cold gusts that hissed through the few stiff tufts of grass growing in the cracks, and it carried grit with it, and the grit ticked against the stones. Below, far below, a valley lay in blue shadow. Up here the sun was hard and white.

{A} came in on the wind. She rode it up the face of the ridge without a wingbeat, tilting, the long feathers at her wingtips spread like fingers, and set down on a flat shelf of stone with a single backward flap that threw dust. She folded her wings. Not all the way.

Across the shelf, in a hollow out of the wind, something uncoiled.

{B} lifted her head out of her own loops slowly, as if she had all day, and her tongue came out and tasted the air in {A}'s direction. Her scales caught the sun in a long rippling line as the coils beneath her shifted. Her eyes were flat and unhurried.

*Ground fighter. Keep off the ground.*

{A}'s wings eased open a hand's width. The wind pressed against them and she let it.

{B}'s head swayed, left, right, following the movement. Then it went very still.

== opening | has_a: fins | has_b: shell
The marsh at dusk smelled of rot and mint. Reeds stood in thick stands round the open water, and the water was black and flat between them, and frogs had started somewhere out of sight, a dry throaty chorus that rose and fell. Mist lay low on the surface. A heron's track, long gone cold, ran across the mud at the water's edge.

{B} was already there. She sat at the edge of the mud like a stone among stones, low and broad, her shell crusted with dried weed, and only her eyes moved, and only slowly.

{A} came up out of the black water a long way out and swam in without a ripple, only the tip of her back fin showing above the surface. She stopped in the shallows, half in and half out, and the water ran off her sleek sides and her fins lifted and spread and caught the last of the light.

The two of them looked at each other across the mud.

*Slow. Heavy. Hard on top. Soft underneath, if I can get there.*

{B}'s head pulled back a little into the shell, not hiding, only setting itself. Her legs pressed into the mud. A frog fell silent close by, and then another, and the mist moved over the water between them.

== opening
There had been a river here once, and now there was only its bed: a long dry trough of pale stones and cracked mud, with the old banks rising on either side, crumbling, held together by roots. The sun stood straight overhead. Nothing threw a shadow worth the name. Heat came up off the stones in a slow wavering shimmer that made the far bank swim.

{A} came down the bank on the near side in a little rush of loose earth and pebbles, and stopped at the bottom, and let the dust settle round her.

{B} was already in the riverbed, a little way along. She had been lying in the one patch of shade under the cut bank, and she rose out of it now without hurry and came out into the light, and the light was so bright on her that for a moment {A} had to narrow her eyes to see her clearly.

They studied each other. {A} took her in from top to bottom, slowly: the set of the shoulders, the way she stood, the stillness of her. {B} did the same, and did not hide that she was doing it.

*She's had the shade. I've had the sun. Doesn't matter.*

Somewhere up the bank, a stone came loose and rattled down and lay still. Neither of them looked at it.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): CHAIN. {A} links blows, each one opening the way for the next.

== chain | kind: claw, bite
{A} went in with her claws first, a hard downward rake across {B}'s shoulder, and did not wait to see what it did.

She knew what it would do. {B}'s head would come round to look at the shoulder, and her guard would follow her head. It did. And as it came round, {A} was already dropping her own head under it, and her jaws were already open.

She bit low, at the base of the neck, where the guard had been a moment before. Her teeth closed. She felt {B} jolt in her grip, the whole body stiffening, and she bore down for one hard heartbeat and let go.

{B} lurched sideways, away from both. Her shoulder was drawn in tight where the claws had raked, the fur there pulled up in three rough furrows. Her neck she held twisted, the bitten side tucked down, and she was shaking her head in short hard jerks as if she could shake the sting out of it.

*Look at the scratch, get the bite. Every time.*

{A} breathed through her open mouth, the taste of {B} on her tongue, and watched the two hurts settle into {B} at once: the gasp that came out of her, the one eye closing, the trembling that started in her shoulder and ran down into her leg.

== chain | has_a: wings, beak | kind: wing, beak
{A} struck with her wing first, a broad flat buffet across {B}'s face that turned her head and filled her eyes with feathers.

It was not meant to hurt much. It was meant to blind. And while {B}'s eyes were still closed against the rush of air and feathers, {A} folded the wing out of the way and her beak went in behind it, sharp and straight, at the side of {B}'s neck that the buffet had turned toward her.

*She never saw the second one. She never sees the second one.*

{A} lifted one wing a fraction, as if to strike again, and watched {B} flinch from the wing and forget the beak entirely.

== chain | has_a: arms | kind: punch
{A} jabbed at {B}'s face, quick and light. Not to hurt. To make her look.

{A} rolled her shoulders and shook out her hands. The knuckles of both were stinging. {B} straightened, slowly, and spat, and a thin pink thread went with it.

{A} dropped onto {B} from above and closed her talons.

{B} stopped twisting. She stood braced under {A}'s weight, head low, breathing hard. {A} could feel the shoulders trembling under her feet, and the heat of the body, and the heartbeat hammering under it.

== grab | kind: bite

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): PUMMEL. {A} holds {B} and strikes again and again at close range.

== pummel | has_a: arms | has_b: mane | kind: punch
{A} had a fistful of {B}'s mane, and she used it.

She dragged {B}'s head down and close and drove her other fist into her side. Short, hard, no room for a full swing, and she did not need one. She drew back a hand's width and hit her again. And again.

{A} worked the side. Ribs, belly, ribs. Every blow knocked a grunt out of {B}, and the grunts were getting shorter, and her body was beginning to fold round {A}'s fist, sagging toward it instead of away.

== pummel | has_a: talons, beak | kind: beak
{A} stood on {B}'s back with her talons sunk in and drove her beak down.

== pummel | has_a: forelegs, tail | kind: tail
{A} pinned {B} with her forelegs and swung her tail.

{B} squirmed under her forelegs. {A} leaned her weight down and kept her there and kept swinging. She did not hurry. Hurrying would have spoiled the aim.

*Every one in the same place. Let it build.*

Before long {B} had stopped squirming and started flinching instead, her whole body jerking under {A}'s legs every time the tail came down, before it came down, as soon as she heard it lift. Her head was pressed to the ground. A cry came out of her with every blow now, the same short cry, cracked at the end.

{A} stopped. Her tail throbbed. She stood over {B}, panting.

{B}'s back was a long welt, the hair or scale or skin there raised and angry and hot. She lay flat and breathing in small sobbing pulls, and every one made her flinch, and her eyes stayed closed.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): ESCAPE. {A} breaks out from under {B}'s pin or hold.

== escape | has_a: wings
{A} had been lying with her wings crushed under {B}, waiting for the weight to shift, and when it shifted she threw them open.

Both at once. They came out from under {B}'s body with a rush of feathers and a wrenching pull at the shoulders, and the first stroke was clumsy, beating against the ground as much as the air. It did not matter. It lifted {B}'s weight a hand's width, and the second stroke lifted it further, and in the space between the two {A} rolled.

She came out sideways in a spray of dust and loose feathers and kept beating, half running, half flying, low over the ground, until there was open air between them.

Then she landed, because her wings would not carry her another stroke, and turned.

Her chest was heaving. Her wings hung open on either side of her, too sore to fold, quills bent and sticking out at angles. Her shoulders burned from the wrench of tearing them free. She stood with her beak open, dragging in air in great whistling gulps.

*Out. Out. Don't ever let her do that again.*

{B} was getting up where she had left her, shaking her head, and {A} watched her and breathed and did not let her eyes leave her for an instant.

== escape | has_a: coils
{B} had her pinned by the middle, and {A} let the rest of her go to work.

Her head and neck were free, and her tail was free, and she used them both. She drew her tail round behind {B}, slow, along the ground, and while {B} was watching her head she hooked the tail round {B}'s leg and pulled.

{B} lurched. Her weight came up off {A}'s middle for a breath, catching herself.

That breath was enough. {A} poured herself out from under. She did not lift; she slid, the whole length of her flowing sideways along the ground, scales whispering on the earth, the pinned part of her dragging free last with a sharp ache where it had been crushed.

She did not stop until she was coiled again, a body's length away, her head up and swaying.

Her middle throbbed. She could feel the flattened place where the weight had been, sore and slow, and when she drew her coils tighter it lagged behind the rest. Her breath came in long uneven swells. Her tongue flickered out, tasting the air.

*Slippery. Remember that. Stay slippery.*

== escape | has_a: flame | kind: fire
{A} could not throw {B} off, so she made {B} want to let go.

She had been pinned a long time, flat on her back, {B}'s weight across her chest. Now she drew in all the breath she could get past that weight, and held it, and let the fire inside her build. She felt it climb. The flame on her body flared higher and hotter until the air above her shook with it.

{B} felt it too. {A} watched her face change, the eyes narrowing against the heat, the head pulling back.

*Hot enough yet? No? Then more.*

{A} let it come. Fire rolled up off her whole body at once, a wash of heat and light, and {B} reared back from it with a yelp, the front of her scorched and smoking, and the weight came off {A}'s chest.

{A} was up in a heartbeat, scrambling, putting distance between them.

She stopped a few lengths away, swaying, her flame guttering low and ragged now that she had spent it. Her chest ached where the weight had been, a deep flat bruise that pulled at every breath. She breathed anyway, in short hard gasps, and her fire breathed with her, small and flickering.

# NEW SAMPLES: FAINT. {B}, pinned and spent, goes out under {A}; told from {A}'s side as the body lets go.

== faint | has_b: wings
{A} felt it through her own body before she saw it.

{B} had been fighting under her the whole time, wings straining against the ground, every muscle of her shoulders bunched and pushing. {A} had held them flat. And now, under her, the pushing stopped.

Not in a jerk. Slowly. She felt the wings go soft beneath her, first the right, then the left, the long feathers settling against the ground like something laid down for the night. The shoulders that had been so hard under her slackened. The trembling that had been running through {B}'s whole body faded, and faded, and was gone.

*Is that it? Is that you done?*

{A} did not let up at once. She held, and watched.

{B}'s head lay turned to one side. Her beak had come open a little, and the breath going in and out of it was slow and even now, very quiet. Her eyes were closed. Her crest, or the feathers on her head, had settled flat and smooth, the way they never had once in the fight.

{A} leaned back. Her own legs were shaking. She stayed where she was a moment longer, over the quiet body with its wings spread out flat beneath her, and listened to it breathe.

== faint | has_b: coils
{A} was holding {B}'s head down, and so she felt the rest of her let go.

It went from the tail first. The far end of {B}'s body had been lashing and curling the whole time, beating against the ground, wrapping and unwrapping. Now it slowed. It curled once more, lazily, and lay still. Then the slack came on up the length of her, loop by loop, every coil easing open and spreading out on the ground until the body under {A} was not coiled at all, only lying.

*Going. Going.*

{B}'s head stopped pushing up against her. {A} felt the neck muscles beneath her go loose, the tension running out of them like water out of cloth.

The tongue slid out a little between the jaws and stayed there.

{A} held on a breath longer to be sure. {B}'s eyes were half open and looked at nothing. Her breath went through her in slow, shallow swells that barely moved her scales, and with each one a faint ripple passed down her length and died.

{A} eased off her. She was trembling herself. She stood looking down at the long, loose body on the ground, her own breath coming hard, and did not quite believe it yet.

== faint | has_b: ears
{A} had been watching {B}'s ears, because they had been telling her everything.

All through the hold they had been flat back against {B}'s skull, pinned there with fury and effort. Every time {B} heaved under her, they flattened harder. Every time she gasped, they twitched.

Now they were rising. Not up, not alert: just loosening, sliding out of their fierce pressed angle, drifting slowly to the sides. The heaving under {A} had grown small. The gasps had grown small too, and further apart.

*She's going.*

{A} kept her weight where it was. She watched one ear flick, once, a small twitch with nothing behind it. Then it settled. Then neither of them moved again.

{B}'s eyes had closed. Her mouth was a little open. Under {A}'s weight her body had gone soft and heavy, all at once, the way a sleeping thing is heavy, and her breath came slow and even and shallow against the ground.

{A} let go. Her own body shook as she lifted off her. She sat back and breathed and looked at the slack ears, and the slack face, and for a long moment did nothing else.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): AFTERMATH. {A} has won; her own body takes stock.

== aftermath | has_a: wings
{A} came down out of the air because there was nothing left to stay up for.

She landed badly, the left wing slow to fold, and stood on the ground with both wings hanging half open and her talons spread to hold her up. It was over. The other one lay a little way off and did not move. {A} looked at her for a long time.

Then she looked at herself.

Her feathers were everywhere wrong. Ruffled, bent, broken here and there, a bare patch on her breast where something had pulled them out. She began, without quite deciding to, to set them right. Her beak went to her shoulder and drew a long feather smooth, and then the next, and the next. Her beak was shaking, a little, and she had to stop now and then and let it.

The left wing hurt when she stretched it. She stretched it anyway, slowly, all the way out, and felt the ache run from the tip to the root and settle there. She folded it in. It went most of the way.

*Done. Done.*

She tucked her head down into her shoulders, and closed her eyes, and breathed, and the wind ruffled the feathers she had not got to yet.

== aftermath | has_a: flame
It was over, and {A}'s fire did not know it.

It was still burning high, the flame on her body standing up tall and hard and roaring, as if there were still something to fight. {A} stood over the ground where it had ended and looked at it and willed it down. It would not go. It flared with every breath she took, and her breath was coming hard and fast.

She made herself breathe slower. In. Hold. Out.

The fire sank a little. She did it again. The roar of it softened to a low steady sound like wind in a chimney, and then to a crackle, and then it was only burning, the way it burned when she was resting.

Her body began to arrive. All of it, now that the fire was not covering it. A deep ache in her side. A sting along one arm. Her legs, which had been holding her up without complaint for the whole fight, began to tremble, and she let herself sink down onto the warm ground and sit.

{B} lay a little way off. {A} watched her chest rise and fall. Rise and fall.

*She'll wake. Not yet.*

The flame on {A} lowered itself, flickering, until it was small and quiet, and she sat in its glow and shut her eyes.

# NEW SAMPLES: AFTERMATH TAKE. {B} lies beaten; the body seen still and breathing.

== aftermath take | has_b: wings
{B} lay where she had come down, on her front with her wings spread out across the ground on either side of her like a dropped cloak.

She had not folded them. She could not. The long feathers lay fanned and crooked in the dirt, some bent, some missing, the gaps between them showing pale. A light wind came across the open ground and lifted the loose ones, and they stirred and settled, and the wings themselves did not stir at all.

Her head was turned to one side, her cheek on the earth. Her beak was a little open. A thread of dust had stuck to the edge of it.

She was breathing. Her breast moved under her, slow and shallow, and each breath ended in a small catch, a hitch, and then went on. Her eyes were closed. Now and again a tremor ran through her from the shoulders out along the wings, so faint that only the feather-tips showed it, quivering, and then they lay still again.

A bird called somewhere off in the trees. She did not hear it. The feathers on her crest stirred in the wind, and settled, and stirred.

== aftermath take | has_b: coils
{B} lay stretched out on the ground in one long loose line, and the coils she had held so tight all fight had come undone.

There was no spiral left in her. She had slid apart where she fell and lay in a slack meander, one bend here, a looser one there, her tail trailing off into the grass. Her head lay at the end of it, on its side, jaw resting on the earth.

The sun lay along her scales. Where they had been scuffed in the fight they were dull, grey with dust; where they had not, they still shone, and the shine moved, very slightly, as she breathed.

That was how {A} could tell. The breath went through her in slow swells, from the head down along the length of her and out to the tail, so faint that it barely moved her, a soft rising of the scales that came and went. In the middle of each one there was a catch, where she had been hurt worst. Then the swell went on.

Her eyes were open a slit and saw nothing. A fly landed on her and walked a little way along her side and flew off, and she did not twitch.

== aftermath take | has_b: fins
{B} lay on the stones at the very edge of the water, where she had been left, and the water kept trying to reach her and not quite reaching.

Small waves came up the shore, thin sheets of it sliding over the pebbles, and touched the tips of her fins, and slid back. Her fins lay spread and loose on the stones. They had been held tight and flared all fight. Now they lay flat, one half folded under her, the other open, its thin edge trembling a little every time the water touched it.

Her skin was drying. {A} could see it dulling in patches where the sun lay on it, the sheen going out of it.

She was breathing, slowly, through her open mouth. Her side lifted and fell. Each breath stopped a little short, caught, and finished, and the next one did the same. Her eyes were closed.

A wave came up further than the others and washed round her, and for a moment her whole body was wet and shining again, and a long shiver went through her fins, out to their edges, as if some part of her knew it. Then the water went back, and the shiver faded, and she lay still.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): CLASH. {A} and {B} strike at the same instant and the two attacks meet.

== clash | kind: fire, water
{A} let the fire go at the same moment {B} let go the water, and the two met in the middle with a sound like a slap on a hot pan.

Steam exploded out of the meeting point. It went up and out in a white boiling wall, and the roar of it drowned everything, and {A} could not see {B} any more, only the white, and could not see her own fire, only feel it pouring out of her into something that ate it.

She pushed. She felt {B} pushing back, out there in the steam, the water coming on heavy and cold and endless. Her flame was being driven back toward her, a hand's width, then another. Heat washed back across her own face.

*Not like this. Not losing to that.*

She dug in and gave it more. The steam thickened. Then the pressure broke all at once on both sides, and the two streams failed together, and the cloud rolled out across the ground and over them both, hot and wet and blinding.

{A} came out of it coughing, her eyes streaming, her face damp and plastered. Somewhere on the other side of the cloud she could hear {B} coughing too, wet and hoarse. Her chest heaved. Her throat burned raw from the fire. She shook her head hard and narrowed her eyes at the thinning white, waiting for a shape in it.

== clash | has_a: horn | has_b: horn | kind: horn
{A} lowered her head and charged, and {B} lowered hers and charged, and they met horn to horn in the middle.

The crack of it went out across the open ground like a tree splitting. {A} felt it in every bone: through the horn into her skull, down her neck, into her shoulders, all the way to her heels. Her vision went white for a heartbeat.

The horns locked. She felt them slide and catch, ridge on ridge, and then they were stuck together and neither of them could pull back.

They pushed. {A} drove with her legs, her whole body behind her head, and felt {B} doing the same, a wall of strength coming the other way. Their faces were a hand apart. She could see {B}'s eyes, wide and white-ringed with effort, and smell her breath, and hear the grunts coming out of both of them at once.

*Give. Give, damn you.*

Neither gave. Her neck burned. Her legs shook. At last they wrenched apart at the same moment, both of them twisting free, and staggered away from each other, heads ringing.

{A} stood swaying with her head low, eyes squeezed shut, the ache in her skull a dull throb that pulsed with her heart. When she opened them, {B} was shaking her own head, slowly, and blinking.

== clash | kind: beam
{A}'s beam left her at the same moment as {B}'s, and they met in the air between them and did not pass.

The two lights struck and pressed, point against point, and where they met a ball of glare swelled up, white and humming, too bright to look at. {A} looked at it anyway, squinting. She could feel it in her chest: the beam pushing out of her, and the other one pushing back, and the shuddering where they met coming down her own beam into her like a current running the wrong way.

She held it. Her whole body leaned into it. Her legs were braced and shaking.

*Hold. She'll break first. Hold.*

The ball of light grew. It sang. And then it burst, and the light went everywhere, and a thump of air knocked into {A}'s face and chest and shoved her back on her heels.

She stood blinking. Purple shapes floated across her eyes. Her chest felt hollow and sore, as if something had been pulled out of it, and her breath came quick and shallow. Across the scorched ground {B} stood in the same state, swaying a little, eyes screwed shut, head turned away from where the light had been.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): DIVE. {A} goes up, folds, and comes down on {B}, or carries her up.

== dive | has_a: wings | has_b: coils | kind: talon
{A} climbed until {B} was a dark coil on the pale ground below, head raised, swaying, watching the sky.

*She sees me. She can't do anything about it.*

{A} folded and fell. The air went from a hum to a howl round her ears, and the ground came up, and the coil grew, and the head on top of it reared higher, jaws opening. {A} let it rear. At the last moment she flared her wings and swung her feet forward under her, talons spread wide, and came down not on the head but behind it, on the thick neck just below the jaw.

Her talons closed. Her weight hit, and kept going, and drove the raised head down into the coils beneath it and the whole stack of {B} flattened under the force.

{A} beat once to steady herself and held.

Under her feet the neck twisted and heaved. The coils thrashed beneath, lashing up round her, but they could not get high enough. She felt the muscle in the neck clench and strain against her grip, felt the hiss going through it, and squeezed her talons tighter.

{B}'s head lay pressed into her own coils, her eyes wide, her tongue flickering frantically, her breath coming in short, sharp hisses through her teeth.

== dive | has_a: wings | kind: wing
{A} did not climb high. She stayed low and came in fast, skimming the ground, her wings spread to their full width.

{B} saw her coming and braced, head down, ready for talons or a beak. {A} had neither in mind. She tipped at the last instant, rolled her body onto its side, and swept past with one wing held rigid, and the hard leading edge of it struck {B} across the side of the head like the flat of a plank.

{A} felt the shock of it run back up the wing to her shoulder, a bright jolt. She was already past, already climbing, her wing stinging.

She turned at the top of her climb and looked back.

{B} was still standing, but her head was down and turned away and she was shaking it slowly, side to side. One eye was squeezed shut. She had swayed with the blow and was still swaying, a little, her balance not quite back. A small welt was rising high on her cheek where the wing had caught her.

*Didn't see it. Never looked for the wing.*

{A} flexed her wing as she circled, opening and closing it, feeling the sting settle into a dull ache at the joint. It still worked.

== dive | has_a: wings, talons | kind: throw
{A} took {B} off the ground and kept climbing.

She had her by the shoulders, talons locked, and she beat with everything she had, and the ground fell away. {B} kicked. She twisted and clawed at the air and screamed, and {A} felt every kick in her own legs and held on and beat harder.

They went up. Up past the tops of the trees, the wind cold and loud. {B}'s weight was dragging on {A}'s legs, pulling her joints straight, and her wings ached with the load, a burning at the shoulders that would not let up.

*High enough. Higher.*

{A} looked down. The ground was small. {B} had stopped kicking. Her legs hung, and her body hung, and {A} could feel her trembling through the talons, a fast fine shake.

She opened her feet.

{B} dropped. {A} watched her fall: the body turning slowly, legs flailing, getting smaller. Then the ground. {A} heard it a heartbeat after she saw it, a dull thud carried up on the wind.

She came down after her in a long spiral, slow, her wings burning, and looked.

{B} lay sprawled where she had landed, on her side, dust settling round her. She was moving, slowly, her legs drawing in. Her breath came in great hoarse heaves. Her eyes were squeezed shut and she did not try to get up.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): KNOCKOUT. {A}'s blow is the last one {B} has room for.

== knockout | has_a: tail | kind: tail
{A} spun and brought her tail round low and flat, and it caught {B} on the side of the head.

She felt it land. Not the hardest blow she had thrown all fight; she had thrown harder. But {B} had nothing left to meet it with. {A} felt the head go with the blow instead of against it, loose, the way a branch goes when it has already been cut.

{B}'s eyes were open as she fell. {A} saw them. They had been fixed on her all fight, sharp, hating, and now they were not fixed on anything. They were just open.

She went down sideways, all at once, legs folding, and hit the ground and did not put out anything to catch herself. She lay where she fell. A small puff of dust drifted off her and settled.

{A} stood with her tail still curled from the swing, breathing hard, and waited for her to move.

She did not move. Her side rose, slowly. Fell. Rose. Her eyes had closed. Her mouth was a little open, and the breath going in and out of it was soft and even, the breath of something deeply asleep.

*Done.*

{A}'s tail uncurled slowly behind her. It ached from the root to the tip, and she let it drag.

== knockout | kind: psychic
{A} reached into {B}'s mind and pushed, and felt it give way all at once.

There had been something there a moment before. Resistance, stubbornness, a hard knot of will that had pushed back against her every time. Now she pushed and there was nothing to push against. It was like leaning on a door and finding it open.

{B} stopped. She did not fall straight away. She stood where she was, swaying, her eyes wide and staring at a point somewhere past {A}'s shoulder. Her mouth opened as if she were going to say something. Nothing came.

Then her eyes rolled up, slowly, and her knees went, and she folded down onto the ground like a cloth slipping off a peg.

{A} let go. Her head was throbbing behind the eyes, a deep sick ache, and she shut her eyes against it and stood breathing.

*Gone. She's gone quiet.*

{B} lay on her side. Her breath came slow and soft. Her face, which had been tight with pain and effort for so long, had gone smooth. Her eyes were closed, and behind the closed lids, now and then, they moved, a small flicker, the way eyes move in sleep.

== knockout | has_b: wings | kind: electric
{A} put everything she had into the bolt and let it go, and it found {B} in the air.

It lit her up. For an instant {A} could see every feather on her, edged in white light, every quill standing out from her body. Her wings went rigid, spread wide and locked. She hung there, stiff as a cut-out, for one heartbeat that seemed much longer.

Then the light went out of her, and so did everything else.

Her wings did not fold. They simply stopped holding the air. {B} dropped out of the sky the way a dropped thing drops, turning slowly, limp, her wings trailing open and loose above her. She did not try to catch herself. {A} watched her fall the whole way down and saw her not try.

She hit the ground on her side with a soft heavy sound and lay still.

{A} let the last of the charge crackle out of her and came closer. Her own body was still humming, her skin buzzing, her heart racing.

{B} lay with one wing spread flat on the ground and the other crumpled under her. Small sparks still ran along her feathers, flickering and dying. Her eyes were closed. Her beak was open a little. And her breast moved, slow and shallow, and kept moving.

*Still breathing. Good. Done.*

# NEW SAMPLES: WATCH. {A} studies what a blow did to {B}: where the hurt sits, what she guards, how her breath goes,
# sized to how hurt the part is and how much of {B} is left.

== watch | has_b: forelegs
{A} held off and looked, because a fresh fighter shows very little, and she did not want to miss the little.

{B} had taken it on the left foreleg, high, near the shoulder. It had not been much of a blow. She was still standing square, head up, her breath deep and steady, and if {A} had glanced away she would have seen nothing at all.

She did not glance away.

There: the foreleg lifted. Only a fraction, the weight eased off it for a breath, the toes barely touching the ground. Then {B} set it down again, deliberately, firmly, as if to prove something to it. The muscle above the elbow gave a single small twitch under the skin and went still.

*Stings. Not more than that. Not yet.*

{B}'s head flicked toward the hurt side once, the way you glance at a door you heard creak. Then her eyes came back to {A}, clear and hard and steady.

That was all. A lift, a twitch, a glance. {A} stored every one of them. The foreleg would be a little slower to turn on now. A little slower to brace. She would lean on the other side when she sprang.

{A} shifted her weight to the left, toward the hurt side, and watched to see which way {B} would turn to meet her.

== watch | has_b: coils
{A} watched {B} draw herself back together, and it took a long time.

The blow had landed in the middle of her length, and the middle was where she was slowest now. The head came up first, then the tail curled in, and the stretch between them lagged, dragging a little behind the rest as if it had to be talked into moving. When it did come, it came in a stiff bent loop, not the smooth curve of the others.

*That part's hurting her. She doesn't want to use it.*

{A} watched the breath go through {B}. It ran in a slow swell from her head toward her tail, and when it reached the struck place it stopped, hitched, and went on smaller. Every breath the same. Every breath a little catch at the same place in her body.

Her tongue flickered out and drew back. Then again, quicker. Her head was not swaying any more; it was holding still, very still, the way a creature holds still when movement hurts.

As {A} watched, a tremor started at the hurt place and ran out in both directions, toward the head and the tail, a little shiver of the scales that took a long time to fade.

{B} was tired. {A} could see it in how low the head sat, a hand's width lower than at the start, and in how the coils sat loose. She was still dangerous. But she was paying for every movement now.

== watch
{A} did not need to go any closer. She could see everything from here, and there was a great deal to see.

{B} was still on her feet, but she was barely holding the shape of a fighter. She stood folded round her own middle, hunched down over the place where the last blow had gone in, holding it close against herself as if it might come loose. Her whole body trembled. Not shivers that came and went: one long, constant trembling that never stopped, from her shoulders to the ground.

Her breath came in small, broken sips. In, catch, out. In, catch, out. On every catch her face pinched tight and something tried to come out of her, a whimper or a moan, and it came out as a thin, broken sound in her throat.

Her eyes were closing. {A} watched them drift shut and stay shut for a long moment, the lids heavy, and then snap open again, as if {B} had caught herself falling asleep on her feet. They found {A}. They lost her. They drifted shut again.

*She's nearly done. She knows it.*

{B} swayed. Forward, a little, and back. Her legs locked to hold her. The hurt place twitched under her, and she sucked in a sharp breath through her teeth and curled tighter round it.

{A}'s own body ached in more places than she could count, but she held perfectly still and kept looking, and did not let any of it into her face.

# NEW SAMPLES: READ. {A} reads {B}'s hurt body for where to strike next.

== read | has_b: wings
{A} circled slowly and read {B} the way you read tracks.

The left wing was the story. {B} kept it folded, but not all the way: there was a small gap at the joint where it would not quite close, a little pucker in the feathers. Every time {B} turned to keep {A} in front of her, that wing stayed tucked tight against her body while the right one opened a little for balance. She was not using the left at all.

*Can't, or won't. Either way.*

{A} watched her breath. {B} breathed through her open beak, quick, and on every breath in the left wing gave a tiny shiver, a tremble at the joint, as if the breathing itself pulled on it.

Her head kept turning toward it. Just a fraction. A glance, and away. She did not know she was doing it.

{A} feinted right. {B} spun to meet it and her left wing jerked half open with the spin, unbidden, and {B}'s whole body flinched, and a small sharp sound came out of her before she could stop it, and the wing clamped back down.

*There it is.*

{A} let the circling go on, slow and patient. She would not hit the wing next. She would make {B} spin. Let the wing do the rest.

== read | has_b: tail
{A} stopped looking at {B}'s face. The tail was telling her more.

All fight it had been moving. Lashing, curling, swinging out for balance on every turn, alive as a separate animal. Now it was not. {B} was holding it still and low and a little out to the side, stiff, the way you hold an arm that has been hurt, keeping it off the ground and away from everything.

{A} watched what happened when {B} turned. The tail had to come round with her. It did, slowly, stiffly, and at the point where it swung furthest {B}'s breath caught in her throat and her eyes narrowed, and she finished the turn short.

*So the tail is where it hurts. And she has to turn to fight.*

{B}'s weight had gone forward a little, too. Away from the tail. Every stance she took now leaned off her hindquarters, as if the tail were an ache she could step away from. It made her front heavy. It made her slow to rear back.

{A} kept her face blank. She tested it: one step to the side, wide. {B} turned to follow, and the tail dragged, and the breath caught again, small, swallowed.

{A} took another step wide. She meant to take a great many.

== read | has_b: arms
{A} watched {B}'s hand, because {B} could not keep it still.

It kept going to her side. Not pressing, not at first; just drifting there and resting, the fingers spread over her lower ribs, and then remembering itself and dropping away. A moment later it would be back. She was not even looking at it. Her hand was keeping watch over the place on its own.

*Ribs. Left side. Low.*

{A} looked at the breath next. {B} was not spent, far from it; she stood well and her eyes were sharp. But the breath was short. It went in a little way and stopped before her chest had finished filling, and went out quickly, and then the next one did the same. She was keeping her breaths small. Big ones hurt.

And when she raised her guard, the left arm came up a little slower than the right, and a little lower, because lifting it stretched the side beneath.

{A} let her own guard drift, as if tired. {B} saw it and shifted to come in, and her weight went onto her left foot, and her hand went to her side again, and stayed there a moment longer than before, the fingers pressing now, before she pulled it away.

*Fresh enough. Hurting more than she wants me to know.*

# NEW SAMPLES: DWELL. {B} lives with the hurt between blows: gripping, breathing, trembling, sized to what is left of her.

== dwell | has_b: wings, feathers
{B} held her wing against her body and waited for it to stop.

It did not stop. The blow had landed at the joint where the wing met her shoulder, and the ache there had a pulse of its own, a deep slow throb that went out along the long bones of the wing to the tips of her flight feathers and came back. She could feel every feather on that side. They were trembling. She could not make them stop.

She tucked her head down against her shoulder, against the hurt place, as if she could hold it still with her own cheek.

Her breath came quick through her open beak. In and out, light and fast and shallow. She did not let herself breathe deep; the deep breaths lifted her shoulders and the shoulder lifted the wing, and the wing woke up and burned.

*Small. Keep it small. Just get through this one.*

Her eyes closed. When they closed, the throbbing got louder, so she opened them again, and the light was too bright, and she let them half close and stood there blinking.

A shiver went through her from the breast up and out along both wings, and lifted every feather she had, and they settled slowly, never quite flat. The hurt wing settled last. She whimpered when it did, a small sound, very quiet, and pressed her cheek harder against her shoulder.

== dwell | has_b: forelegs
{B} kept the foreleg off the ground and stood on the others, and that was all she could manage for now.

It hung bent under her chest, the paw curled, not touching anything. She did not dare let it touch. The blow had gone in just above the wrist and the whole leg had become one long ache from the paw to the shoulder, and it throbbed with every beat of her heart: up, down, up, down, steady as a drum.

She lowered her head toward it. She licked it once, the fur over the hurt place, and the touch of her own tongue made the leg jerk and she sucked in a breath through her teeth.

*Leave it. Leave it alone.*

She stood lopsided and breathed. Her breaths were short and came through her nose in hard little puffs, and each one shook her slightly, and the shaking went down into the lifted leg and set it trembling in the air.

She tried putting it down. Just the tips of the toes. The ground was there, solid, and the moment her weight began to go onto it the ache leapt up white and she snatched it back with a gasp.

Her eyes squeezed shut. She stood very still on her good legs, swaying a little, her lifted paw shaking, waiting for the leg to quiet enough that she could think.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): PIN START. {A} gets {B} down and settles her weight to keep her there.

== pin start | has_a: wings, talons
{B} was down on her side, and {A} dropped on her from above before she could roll.

She came down with her talons first. One foot took {B} at the shoulder and the other at the hip, and {A} let her whole weight follow them and pressed, and felt the body under her flatten against the ground. Her wings stayed spread wide, half beating, holding her balance as {B} bucked.

{B} bucked hard. {A} rode it. Her talons tightened and she leaned forward into the shoulder, and the next buck came smaller, and the one after that smaller still.

*Down. You're staying down.*

She folded her wings partway, slowly, and let them drape down on either side of the two of them, a tent of feathers that shut out the light. Under it, {B}'s breath was loud and fast, rasping against the earth.

{A} adjusted her grip. She shifted one foot a little higher on the shoulder, where the muscle was thinner and would not give her room to push, and felt {B} flinch under it.

Her own legs were shaking with the strain. She locked them. She looked down at {B}'s face, pressed sideways into the dirt, one eye rolling up to find her, and held her gaze.

== pin start | has_a: shell
{A} was slow, and she knew it, so she did not chase. She waited for {B} to fall, and when {B} fell, she walked onto her.

She did it heavily, deliberately, one foot and then the next, and lowered her shell down across {B}'s middle like a stone settling into a riverbed. {B} grunted as the weight came onto her. A long, hard push of air out of her, and then a wheeze.

{A} drew her legs in a little and let the shell do the work.

{B} pushed up against it. {A} felt it as a faint rocking, nothing more. She was built heavy and low and wide, and there was nothing under her that could lift her.

*Push. Wear yourself out on it.*

{B} pushed again, and again, and the rocking got smaller. {A} put her head out and turned it to look at {B}'s face. It was close, a hand's width from her own, screwed up tight with effort. The breath going in and out of it was short and squeezed. Every time {B} tried for a full breath, the shell was in the way, and the breath stopped halfway, and {B} gasped.

{A} settled lower. She could be patient. She had always been made for patience.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): RESISTED ACT. {A}'s attack meets a body made to take it.

== resisted act | kind: grass | has_b: flame
{A} sent the vines at her, and the vines did what vines do near fire.

They went out of her in a green rush and whipped round {B}'s legs and up her body, and for a heartbeat {A} thought it would work. Then {B}'s flame flared. It did not even look deliberate. It simply rose, the way fire rises when something is fed to it, and the vines touched it and blackened.

{A} felt it through them. A shrivelling sting that came back up the vines into her and made her gasp. The green went brown and the brown went black, and the vines curled and crumbled and fell away from {B} in smoking pieces.

{B} had hardly moved. There were a few dark marks on her where the vines had lashed, a little welt or two. That was all. She shook herself and the last of the ash fell off her.

*That was supposed to hold her.*

{A} pulled what was left of her vines back in. They came back limp and scorched and stinging. She stood breathing, her face hot, and looked at {B}, and {B}'s flame burned on as if nothing had happened, and her eyes, over it, were almost amused.

== resisted act | kind: poison
{A} spat the poison at {B} and watched it fail.

It struck her full on the side. A thick dark spray, the kind that burned on contact, that should have had her hissing and scrubbing at it. {A} waited for that.

It did not come. The poison hit and clung and did nothing. It beaded on her like rain on wax, slid down her side in slow thick drops, and dripped off onto the ground, where the grass under it browned and withered. {B} herself only twitched. A small shiver of the skin where it had landed, as if a fly had touched her. She looked down at it. She looked up again.

*She doesn't even feel it.*

{A}'s throat ached from the effort of bringing it up. Her mouth tasted bitter. She swallowed, and the taste stayed.

{B} took a step toward her across the withered grass, unhurried, and the last drops of poison fell off her and hissed on the ground.

{A} did not step back. But she wanted to, and she felt the wanting, and for a moment her mind was entirely empty of what to try next.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): SUPER EFFECTIVE ACT. {A} finds the attack {B}'s body has no answer for.

== super effective act | has_b: fins | kind: electric
{B} had come up out of the water to fight, and she was still dripping.

{A} saw the water running off her fins and pooling round her feet and felt the charge in her own body rise to meet it, eager, like a dog that has smelled something. She did not even have to aim carefully. She let it go.

The bolt went into {B} and then it went everywhere. Through the wet skin, along every fin, down into the puddle at her feet and back up again. {B}'s fins snapped out rigid, every spine standing up stiff and shaking. Her whole body locked. Her mouth opened wide and no sound came out of it at all.

*That's it. That's the thing she can't take.*

{A} cut it off. {B} did not go down. She stood where she was with her fins still sticking out from her body, trembling, and her legs shaking under her, and her eyes squeezed tight shut. Then a sound came out of her, at last: a long, thin, broken cry. Steam rose faintly off her wet skin.

Her fins folded, slowly, one by one, and the last one would not fold. It hung half open, twitching.

{A} felt the next charge already gathering in her, prickling at her skin, and did not hurry.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): THROW. {A} heaves {B} off the ground and lets her go.

== throw | has_a: horn | kind: horn, throw
{A} got her horn under {B}'s middle as {B} came in, and lifted.

It was the whole of her. Her neck, her shoulders, her legs straightening under her, all of it going up through the horn at once. {B} came off the ground. {A} felt her weight on the horn, heavy, swinging, the body draped across it for an instant with its legs kicking at the air.

{A} tossed her head.

{B} went up and over, turning in the air, and came down behind {A} on her back with a heavy thud that shook the ground. Dust rose round her.

{A} turned. Her neck burned from the toss, a deep pulling ache at the base of the skull, and the horn itself was humming faintly with the weight it had carried.

{B} lay on her back, legs in the air, stunned. Her eyes were wide and staring up at nothing. Her mouth opened and closed. She rolled, slowly, onto her side, and lay there with her belly heaving, a long groan coming out of her, and did not get any further.

*Lighter than she looks.*

{A} lowered her head again and scraped the ground once and waited.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): PIN HOLD. {A} keeps {B} down and feels her own body pay for it.

== pin hold | has_a: coils
{A} lay across {B} in heavy loops, and held her down by being heavy.

She was not squeezing. She did not need to. Her body was long and thick and it lay over {B}'s legs and hips and chest in broad, slack bends, and every bend weighed as much as a log. {B} could breathe. She could not get up.

{A} raised her head above it all and watched.

{B} lay still under her, mostly. Every so often a push came up through {A}'s coils, a shove of the hips, a heave of the shoulders. {A} felt it as a ripple through her scales and simply let her weight settle a little lower after it, into the room the push had made.

*Wear yourself down. I can lie here all day.*

That was not quite true. Her own middle ached where {B} had struck it earlier, and lying across the hard ground pressed the ache flat and made it throb. Her breathing ran in long, slow swells, and each one caught at that sore place, and she let it catch and did not let it show.

{B}'s face was turned up toward her. The eyes were open, fierce, wet at the corners. The breath came in and out of {B}'s mouth quick and shallow, held small under the weight.

{A}'s tongue flickered out, tasting the heat of her, and drew back in.

# NEW SAMPLES: PIN STRUGGLE. {B}, pinned under {A}, fights the weight with what she can move.

== pin struggle | has_b: wings
{B} could not lift her body, so she tried with her wings.

They were pinned at the shoulders where {A}'s weight was heaviest, but the long ends of them were free, spread out across the ground on either side, and she beat them. Hard. Flat against the earth, a great slapping beat that kicked up dust and grass and feathers and lifted {A}'s weight by a hair.

Only a hair. {A} settled again and the wings beat again and the dust went up again.

*Up. Up. Get up.*

It was not working. Every beat pulled at her shoulders where they were trapped, a deep wrenching pull at the joints, and every beat lifted her less. Her wings were getting heavy. Her feathers were full of dirt.

She stopped, panting through her open beak, her wings lying spread and shaking at the tips.

{A} shifted above her. {B} felt it, felt the weight move toward her head, and threw herself the other way, twisting her body under it toward her hip with everything she had left. Her talons scrabbled at the earth. A long cry came out of her, cracked, furious.

The weight came back down. She was still pinned. Her chest heaved against the ground, and a shiver ran out along both wings from the shoulders to the tips, and the long feathers trembled and lay still.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): GETUP. {A} gets herself up off the ground and back into the fight.

== getup | has_a: coils
{A} lay in a loose spill of herself on the ground and began, slowly, to gather.

The tail first. She drew it in, a long slow curl, and the movement woke an ache in her middle that made her stop and breathe. Then the next loop, closer. Then the next. Each one came a little stiffer than the last, and in the middle of her, where the worst blow had landed, the coils did not want to bend at all.

She made them.

When she had herself drawn into a rough pile, she began to lift her head. It came up a hand's width and wobbled there, and her neck trembled holding it, and she let it sink back down onto her own coils and rest.

*Again. Higher.*

She lifted it again. This time she kept it coming, slowly, her neck stiffening under it, until her head was up above her coils where it belonged. It swayed. She let it sway. Her tongue flickered out, tasting the air.

Her whole length was humming with small aches, bruised places all along her, and her breath swelled through her in long uneven pulls. But she was up. She tightened her coils under her, slowly, testing them, and turned her head to find {B}.

# NEW SAMPLES (acting side): DODGE. {A} gets out of the way of {B}'s attack.

== dodge | has_a: wings | has_b: wings
{B} came at her through the air, fast and straight, and {A} waited until she could see the whites of her eyes.

Then she folded one wing.

Only one. The other stayed open, and the open wing caught the air and the closed one did not, and she fell sideways out of the sky like a stone dropped off a ledge. {B} went through the place she had been. {A} felt the rush of her passing, a gust that ruffled every feather on her back, and heard something snap shut on empty air.

She opened the folded wing again a body's length lower, and the air caught her, and she was level and climbing.

*Too eager. Always too eager.*

Her heart was pounding. Her shoulder ached from the sudden snap of the fold. She looked back and saw {B} trying to turn, wide and clumsy, carried on past by her own speed, losing height.

{A} beat hard and rose above her. Her breath was quick through her open mouth, and her wings felt light and strong and hers. She banked into a slow circle and watched {B} come round, and waited for her to try again.
